±2.50%, written April 2023 to September 2026. **97.5% thumbs up** in the sample (Steam, all languages:
98%, Overwhelmingly Positive).
**Pages:** `findings/crab-champions-english.md`, `findings/crab-champions.md`, `findings/cross-game.md`
section 24.

**What it is:** A third-person roguelike shooter built on runs. You play alone or with friends online.
The pages do not give the largest group size (one reviewer claims 40 friends, 232914887, not checked)
and do not say who hosts. A run hops from island to island, you stack power-ups, and you can loop on and
on (runs of 2 to 3 hours, up to island 240, reviewers say). Cartoon crabs, no sci-fi, no extraction.
Paid (the pages do not give the price; reviewers call it fair). Made and published by Noisestorm, the
musician behind *Crab Rave*. In Early Access on Steam since 2023-04-01; every sampled review was written
in Early Access. Steam lists 11 Early Access updates from 2023-04 to 2025-11. The engine is not named on
the pages.

**How close to Dominion:** It shares the third-person camera, online co-op and runs that build power. It
differs in its cartoon, joking look, no sci-fi, no extraction, and (as far as the pages say) nothing much
carrying over between runs.

### The short version (plain words)
Almost nobody dislikes it: 97.5% thumbs up and only 18.7 complaints per 100 reviews, second lowest of
the games counted this way. But most reviews say little; 61 of every 100 are a thumbs up with nothing
specific, often just "crab". When players do say why, it is about feel: quick movement (39 times),
feeling far too strong (37), and playing with friends (86, rising from 4.1 to 10.0 per 100). The one
update that split fans was the Island Update of 2025-11-02: complaints rose from 12.7 to 24.8 per 100
after it, while thumbs up stayed at 97.8%.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (bullets). 915 more thumbs-up reviews said nothing specific (61.0 per 100,
the highest in the corpus).
1. **Much better with friends.** Said 86 times (5.7 per 100). Rose from 4.1 per 100 at launch to 10.0
   after the Island Update.
2. **Keeps pulling you back.** Said 78 times (5.2 per 100).
3. **The price is fair.** Said 69 times (4.6 per 100). One 2024-10 reviewer says it is *"at a quarter of
   the price"* of Risk of Rain 2 (177078102).
4. **Best game of its kind.** Said 42 times (2.8 per 100).
5. **Builds are deep and varied.** Said 41 times (2.7 per 100).
6. **The music fits the game.** Said 39 times (2.6 per 100). Fell from 3.5 to 0.9 per 100 over time.
7. **Movement feels quick and responsive.** Said 39 times (2.6 per 100). Fell from 3.2 to 1.3 per 100
   over time. Reviews praising movement are 2.7 per 100 here and 2.6 in Warframe; every other English
   group of 300 or more reviews is at 0.5 or less.
8. **You get to feel far too strong.** Said 37 times (2.5 per 100). Rose from 1.5 to 3.9 per 100. Only
   Space Marine 2 (4.5) and Risk of Rain 2 (3.8) are higher.
9. **A steady stream of updates.** Said 24 times (1.6 per 100).
10. **Combat hits hard.** Said 22 times (1.5 per 100).
11. **Looks like a joke, plays like a real game.** Said 20 times (1.3 per 100); second in the corpus to
    Escape from Duckov (3.4).
12. **Hard in a good way.** Said 20 times (1.3 per 100). Rose from 0.3 to 2.2 per 100.
13. **Works well solo.** Said 19 times (1.3 per 100), against 1 bullet that calls it punishing alone.
14. **It looks great.** Said 16 times (1.1 per 100).
15. **Plenty of achievements to hunt.** Said 16 times (1.1 per 100).
16. **Difficulty can be tuned in detail.** Said 16 times (1.1 per 100), the highest in the corpus
    (Escape from Duckov 0.5). Rose from 0.0 to 2.2 per 100.
17. **Wants more because it is good.** Said 14 times (0.9 per 100).
18. **Every weapon or option plays differently.** Said 14 times (0.9 per 100).
19. **Easy to pick up.** Said 13 times (0.9 per 100).
20. **Plenty of content.** Said 13 times (0.9 per 100).
21. **Made with care.** Said 12 times (0.8 per 100).
22. **The base design works and more can still come.** Said 12 times (0.8 per 100).
23. **An update made it better.** Said 12 times (0.8 per 100).
24. **The art style works.** Said 12 times (0.8 per 100).
25. **The run can loop on and on.** 7 (only in this game).
26. **The studio listens and acts.** 5; 3 of them from 2025-11 and 2025-12. *"An amazing developer who
    listens and keeps updating it"* (209872720).
27. **Co-op is rare in this kind of game.** 3 (only in this game). *"Co-op, which is rare for the
    genre"* (142668300).
28. **Teammates can trade items.** 2.
29. **Online play is smooth.** 2. A dropped player kept the run (1; the save kept it).
30. **Came because of the musician.** 3.

Neither praise nor complaint: 53 times reviewers described it by naming other games. 23 reviews name
*Crab Rave*. The most-helpful review is *"I wish crabs were real"* (158886230, 781 helpful); the second
is the pun *"Risk of Rangoon"* (136295436, 396 helpful).

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said (bullets). The list is long and thin; no complaint reaches 1 per 100.
**182 of the 280 complaint bullets (65.0%) come from reviews that still recommend the game.** Only 37
reviews are thumbs down, and they have a median of 17.5 hours played (all reviews: 12.7).
1. **Thumbs down with no reason given.** 13 (0.9 per 100).
2. **Too little content.** 9 (0.6 per 100).
3. **An expected mode is missing.** 8 (0.5 per 100). 5 of the 8 want PvP, duels or teams.
4. **Runs out fast** (you have seen it all quickly). 8 (0.5 per 100).
5. **Luck decides whether you win.** 7 (0.5 per 100).
6. **An update made it worse.** 7 (0.5 per 100); 3 after 2025-11, plus a 2025-02 review edited
   2025-11-12 that blames patch 11.0.
7. **One enemy type is hated.** 6 (0.4 per 100).
8. **Your power outgrows the challenge.** 6 (0.4 per 100). *"After a couple of loops you are so
   overpowered that going on forever feels pointless"* (150108738).
9. **Updates are too slow.** 6 (0.4 per 100); 4 of them in 2026.
10. **Enemies are bullet sponges.** 5 (0.3 per 100).
11. **An attack you cannot dodge or answer.** 5 (0.3 per 100).
12. **One option is far stronger than the rest.** 5 (0.3 per 100).
13. **Two achievements give the same reward.** 5 (0.3 per 100; only in this game). 1 more says the best
    skin is nearly impossible to get.
14. **Favourite items were weakened.** 5 (0.3 per 100); 2 after 2025-11.
15. **The game slows down at the highest levels.** 5 (0.3 per 100; only in this game). 3 of the 5 call
    it a reward: *"The joy is a build that drops the frame rate below 30"* (234681682).
16. **Wants a talent tree or upgrades between runs.** 4 (only in this game). 1 more says losing
    everything makes play feel worthless.
17. **The hitboxes are off.** 4 (only in this game).
18. **The balance assumes you restart until you get a god build.** 4 (only in this game).
19. **A feature was removed.** 4 (3 are the old islands; 1 is keys, 2023-11).
20. **Enemies spawn with no chance to react.** 3.
21. **Cannot tell what is attacking.** 3.
22. **The song the game is built on is missing** (*Crab Rave*). 3 (only in this game).
23. **Ammo, health or supplies too scarce.** 3.
24. **Badly scaled difficulty steps.** 3.
25. **Bosses are just bigger enemies.** 3 (only in this game).
26. **Too few kinds of island.** 3 (only in this game).
27. **Wants the old version back.** 3 (only in this game).
28. **The map edge cannot be seen.** 2 (built here).
29. **An unkillable snail hunts you.** 2 (built here).
30. **The gun is fixed for the whole run.** 2 want to change it or carry a second; one gun gets boring
    after many loops (only in this game).
31. **Wants split screen.** 2.
32. **Made harder for hardcore players at casual players' cost.** 1 (151186055, 2023-11).
33. **Small co-op ones:** unclear who gets the loot (1); one save slot shared by solo and co-op (1); a
    disconnect lost the run (1); matchmaking (2).

### How it changed over time
Four periods. Margins are about ±5, ±5, ±4 and ±7, so read for direction.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Launch (April 2023) | 340 | 98.2% | 48.5 | 18.8 |
| First year (May-Dec 2023) | 411 | 96.6% | 62.5 | 19.5 |
| Steady Early Access (2024 to Oct 2025) | 518 | 97.7% | 66.8 | 12.7 |
| After the Island Update (Nov 2025 to Sept 2026) | 230 | 97.8% | 71.3 | 24.8 |

- **Praise grows every period.** Friends 4.1 → 10.0; feeling too strong 1.5 → 3.9; hard in a good way
  0.3 → 2.2; difficulty you can tune 0.0 → 2.2.
- **Some praise fades.** Music 3.5 → 0.9; quick movement 3.2 → 1.3.
- **The launch says least**: 62.9 of every 100 launch reviews are a bare thumbs up.
- **The Island Update (2025-11-02, checked against Steam's news)** replaced every island, swapped the
  blue boundary walls for a new out-of-bounds system, and reworked difficulty with a new top rank,
  Prismatic. Hotfixes on 2025-11-03 and 2025-11-22 widened the out-of-bounds zones, made the Immortal
  Snail purple so it is easier to see, and moved two modifiers off the medal path after feedback. On
  2026-03-18 the developer said the next update had no firm date.
- **After it, complaints doubled (12.7 → 24.8 per 100) while thumbs up held at 97.8%.** People complain
  and still recommend it. Led by missing modes, slow updates and "it made it worse".
- **The longest complaint is a thumbs down from a fan** (226203004, 2026-05). The old islands each had a
  gimmick; the new ones *"feel like simple arenas"*; the out-of-bounds teleport is *"an awful idea"*; it
  now takes too little time to become unstoppable. It asks for a harder game with fewer upgrades, not
  more content.
- **Hardcore against casual.** The developer's own 2025-11-22 note splits feedback into players who want
  a fair new challenge and players who want the hardest setting possible.
- **New rivals in 2026:** Megabonk named in 2 reviews; one thumbs down wishes it had *"the same dev
  attention as like megabonk"* (229868355).

### Co-op and online play
- **It is a friends game, more each year:** better with friends 86, rising 4.1 → 10.0 per 100.
- **Solo works too:** 19 against 1.
- **Co-op is part of the pitch:** 3 reviews call co-op rare in this genre.
- **Online play draws almost no complaints.** In 1,499 reviews: 1 bullet on a disconnect that lost the
  run, 1 on a disconnect that did not (the save kept it), 2 on matchmaking, 2 on smooth online play, 2
  naming online play without saying what.
- **Loot:** teammates can trade items (2, praised); who gets the loot is unclear (1).
- **Saves:** one save slot for solo and co-op together (1).
- **Joining:** one review asks for the invite to drop a friend straight into the game (226199324).
- **Split screen:** 2 want it.
- Group size: one reviewer claims 40 friends (232914887), not checked. The pages say nothing about who
  hosts, bots, friendly fire or crossplay.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Movement is the feel players name.** Quick, responsive movement 39 (2.6 per 100). Only Warframe
  matches it; elsewhere the commonest movement complaint is "sluggish".
- **Feeling far too strong is praise:** 37, rising 1.5 → 3.9 per 100.
- **Combat hits hard:** 22. **Every option plays differently:** 14.
- **Complaints:** hitboxes off (4); attacks you cannot answer (5); cannot tell what is attacking (3).
- **Frame rate drops at the top** (5), and 3 of those enjoy it as a sign they won.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Difficulty you set yourself** is praised: tuned in detail 16 (highest in the corpus); hard in a good
  way 20, rising 0.3 → 2.2 per 100.
- **Enemy complaints are small:** one enemy type hated 6; bullet sponges 5; no counterplay 5; unfair
  spawns 3; threats unclear 3; bosses just bigger enemies 3; badly scaled steps 3.
- **The Immortal Snail**: an unkillable enemy that hunts you (2). The developer made it purple to see it
  better.
- **Power outgrows the challenge** (6); one reviewer says the danger rises with how far you go, not with
  a clock as in Risk of Rain (175059509).
- **Hardcore vs casual:** made harder for the dedicated at casual players' cost (1, 151186055); the
  developer saw the same split.

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Inside a run:** builds deep and varied 41; you stack power-ups and loop.
- **Luck:** luck decides the outcome 7; the balance assumes you restart for a god build 4.
- **Between runs:** nothing much carries over. 4 want a talent tree or upgrades between runs; 1 says
  losing everything makes play feel worthless. One reviewer says only new weapons and cosmetics carry
  over (203287249, not checked).
- **One gun per run:** 2 want to swap or carry a second.
- **Achievements:** plenty to hunt 16; two give the same skin 5; best skin nearly impossible 1.
- No grind complaints are counted on the pages.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **Keeps pulling you back:** 78.
- **The run can loop on and on:** 7. Runs last 2 to 3 hours; island 240 is named.
- **Against it:** power outgrows the challenge 6; runs out fast 8; too little content 9; too few kinds of
  island 3.
- **For it:** plenty of content 13; wants more because it is good 14.
- **Waiting is part of liking it:** awaiting promised content 27; steady stream of updates 24.

### Money and price
- **Fair price:** 69 (4.6 per 100). One reviewer says it costs a quarter of Risk of Rain 2 (177078102).
- No shop, DLC or battle pass complaints are named on the pages. The publishing side is only 3.4% of
  what players say.

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- **Engineering is only 2.7% of what players say.** Online play, crashes and frame rate draw few
  complaints.
- **Slows at the highest levels:** 5, and 3 of them enjoy it.
- **Hitboxes off:** 4.
- **The map edge cannot be seen** after the Island Update (2).
- The pages name no crashes or save losses beyond the 1 disconnect.

### The studio, updates and community
- **One developer, updates every few months.** 11 Early Access updates from 2023-04 to 2025-11 (Steam).
- **Steady stream** 24; **too slow** 6 (4 in 2026); **awaiting promised content** 27.
- **Updates made it better** 12; **made it worse** 7; **weakened favourite items** 5; **removed the old
  islands** 3; **wants the old version back** 3.
- **Listens and acts:** 5. The developer moved two modifiers off the medal path after feedback and made
  the snail easier to see (Steam news).
- **Promised modes:** 2 launch reviews want the parkour and one-on-one modes an earlier trailer showed.
- **Risk of Rain 2 is the yardstick:** 43 reviews (2.9%) name it, 2 of them thumbs down. All 9 "beats its
  rivals" bullets are against it: *"Risk of Rain but good"* (137524432), *"Risk of Rain if it were fun"*
  (150154818). Two 2024-10 reviews came because Risk of Rain 2 got worse for them under its new owner
  (177078102, 178129296). One says Risk of Rain 2's expansion *"should have been this"* (186213805). The
  2 thumbs down that name it call it a copy (154240327) or say to play Risk of Rain 2 to finish
  everything (157234469). Gunfire Reborn is named twice.
- **Culture:** reviews are short jokes; many say only "crab". 23 name *Crab Rave*; 3 came for the
  musician, 3 miss the song.

### What players asked for
- **PvP, duels or teams:** 5 of the 8 missing-mode complaints.
- **An extraction version:** 1 (*"PVP Version or Extraction Shooter Version"*, 230547405).
- **Split screen:** 2.
- **A talent tree or upgrades between runs:** 4.
- **A second gun or a gun swap in a run:** 2.
- **The old islands back:** 3.
- **The modes shown in a trailer** (parkour, one-on-one): 2.
- **The *Crab Rave* song in the game:** 3.
- **Faster updates:** 6.
- **An invite that drops a friend straight in:** 1 (226199324).
- **A harder game with fewer upgrades:** 1 (226203004).

### Only in this game
52 tags appear in no other game. The ones that matter for design:
- The run can loop on and on: 7 (praise).
- Two achievements give the same reward: 5.
- The game slows down at the highest levels: 5 (and most of those like it).
- The balance assumes you restart for a god build: 4.
- Wants a talent tree between runs: 4.
- Hitboxes off: 4.
- Co-op is rare in its genre: 3 (praise).
- Came for the creator's other work: 3.
- Wants the old version back: 3.
- Too few kinds of island: 3.
- Bosses are just bigger enemies: 3.
- The song it is built on is missing: 3.
- Also built here: the map edge cannot be seen (2), an unkillable enemy hunts you (2), the gun is fixed
  for the run (2).

### For Dominion - our reading
1. **How it feels to move is judged by name. Make movement quick.**
   Evidence: quick movement praised 39 times (2.6 per 100). Reviews praising movement are 2.7 per 100
   here and 2.6 in Warframe; every other large group is at 0.5 or less, and "sluggish" is the common
   complaint elsewhere. The cross-game page says: in a third-person shooter, a slide, dash or jump that
   feels quick is praised by name, and a slow one is the complaint.
   Dominion could: make sprint, slide, dodge and jump snappy, with little delay, and test them early with
   players.
   Strength: strong.
2. **Let players get absurdly strong inside a run.**
   Evidence: feeling far too strong praised 37 times, rising 1.5 → 3.9 per 100 (Risk of Rain 2 3.8);
   builds deep and varied 41; players enjoy the frame rate dropping as the build explodes (3 of 5).
   Dominion could: let run power stack into big, visible effects near the end of a run.
   Strength: strong.
3. **But keep the danger rising with the power, or end the run.**
   Evidence: power outgrows the challenge 6 (*"going on forever feels pointless"*, 150108738); runs out
   fast 8; balance assumes restarting for a god build 4; the fan's thumbs down asks for a harder game
   with fewer upgrades (226203004). One reviewer likes that danger rises with distance, not a clock
   (175059509).
   Dominion could: tie difficulty to how deep the run goes; use extraction as the natural end so a run
   does not go stale; make normal builds win, not only god builds.
   Strength: medium.
4. **Big effects must not kill the host's frame rate.**
   Evidence: slows at the highest levels 5 (only in this game). Most enjoy it here, but Dominion is
   hosted on one player's machine.
   Dominion could: cap effects and projectile counts, and merge damage numbers, so a strong build does
   not lag the host and every client.
   Strength: weak (small count), but the risk is larger on a listen server.
5. **Co-op is the reason people stay, and solo must still work.**
   Evidence: better with friends 86, rising 4.1 → 10.0 per 100; works solo 19 against 1; co-op called
   rare in its genre 3.
   Dominion could: put co-op at the center of the pitch and keep solo runs fair.
   Strength: strong.
6. **Protect the run when someone drops, and make joining one click.**
   Evidence: online play draws almost no complaints, but 1 lost a run on a disconnect and 1 kept it
   because of the save; 1 asks for an invite that drops a friend straight in (226199324); 1 has one save
   slot for solo and co-op.
   Dominion could: save run state so a dropped player can rejoin; let invites join a run in progress;
   keep solo and co-op saves clear.
   Strength: weak to medium.
7. **Be clear about who gets the loot, and let teammates share.**
   Evidence: trading items praised 2; who gets the loot unclear 1.
   Dominion could: show on screen who a drop is for, and allow trades inside the team.
   Strength: weak.
8. **Many difficulty dials win praise.**
   Evidence: difficulty tuned in detail 16 (highest in the corpus), rising 0.0 → 2.2 per 100; hard in a
   good way 20, rising 0.3 → 2.2. The developer saw fans split between a fair challenge and the hardest
   possible; one review says it was made harder for the dedicated at casual players' cost (151186055).
   Dominion could: offer separate dials (enemy count, damage, modifiers) and a top rank for the
   hardcore, without making the normal game harder for everyone.
   Strength: medium.
9. **A big update that removes loved content splits fans.**
   Evidence: after the Island Update complaints went 12.7 → 24.8 per 100; made it worse 7; old islands
   removed 3; wants the old version back 3; map edge cannot be seen 2; unkillable snail hunting you 2;
   the fan review says new maps *"feel like simple arenas"*.
   Dominion could: add new maps beside old ones rather than replace them; give each map its own gimmick;
   make map edges easy to see; test hunter-type enemies before shipping.
   Strength: medium.
10. **Some players want something to carry between runs.**
    Evidence: 4 want a talent tree between runs; 1 says losing everything makes play feel worthless; only
    weapons and cosmetics carry over, one reviewer says (203287249).
    Dominion could: let extraction bank something lasting (unlocks, a light talent tree), as Dominion
    plans.
    Strength: weak to medium.
11. **One fixed gun per run gets boring after many loops.**
    Evidence: 2 want to swap guns or carry a second.
    Dominion could: allow at least one weapon swap or a second slot in a run.
    Strength: weak.
12. **Do not repeat rewards across achievements.**
    Evidence: two achievements give the same reward 5; best skin nearly impossible 1; plenty to hunt 16.
    Dominion could: give each challenge its own reward and keep the hardest reachable.
    Strength: weak.
13. **Make threats readable.**
    Evidence: attacks you cannot answer 5; enemies spawn with no time to react 3; cannot tell what is
    attacking 3; hitboxes off 4; the developer recoloured the snail to make it easier to see.
    Dominion could: give each attack a clear tell, keep spawns out of sight lines near players, and match
    hitboxes to models.
    Strength: weak to medium.
14. **Make bosses more than bigger enemies.**
    Evidence: bosses just bigger enemies 3; one enemy type hated 6; bullet sponges 5.
    Dominion could: give each boss its own moves and rules.
    Strength: weak.
15. **A fair price earns goodwill.**
    Evidence: fair price 69; one reviewer says it costs a quarter of Risk of Rain 2.
    Dominion could: price low enough that friends will buy it together.
    Strength: medium.
16. **In Early Access, a steady update rhythm is praised and silence is noticed.**
    Evidence: steady stream 24; awaiting promised content 27; too slow 6 (4 in 2026); the developer said
    the next update had no firm date (2026-03-18).
    Dominion could: post a simple roadmap and keep a regular rhythm; say early when a date slips.
    Strength: medium.
17. **Do not show modes in a trailer you will not ship.**
    Evidence: 2 launch reviews want the parkour and one-on-one modes an earlier trailer showed.
    Dominion could: show only what is coming soon.
    Strength: weak.
18. **Some co-op players want PvP or extraction next to the main game.**
    Evidence: 5 of 8 missing-mode complaints want PvP, duels or teams; 1 asks for an extraction version
    (230547405); 2 want split screen.
    Dominion could: note the interest; it does not mean PvP belongs in Dominion.
    Strength: weak.
19. **Music can carry identity, but it fades as a talking point.**
    Evidence: music fits 39, falling 3.5 → 0.9 per 100; 3 miss the creator's song.
    Dominion could: give runs a strong sound identity.
    Strength: weak.
20. **A rival that slips sends players your way.**
    Evidence: 43 reviews name Risk of Rain; all 9 "beats its rivals" bullets are against it; 2 came
    because Risk of Rain 2 got worse for them.
    Dominion could: know which game it will be compared to and what that game's players miss.
    Strength: weak to medium.

### Limits
- The sample is 5.6% of English reviews, ±2.50% on whole-sample shares; the periods carry about ±4 to
  ±7. The after-update period is only 230 reviews (about ±7): the direction is clear, the size is not.
- Two months carry the most weight: the launch (2023-04) and 2025-03, each ×20.
- 41% of what was said is nothing specific; specific counts are small and no complaint reaches 10.
- 89 reviews (5.9%) were edited later and counted on the date they were made, so a comment on the Island
  Update can sit in an earlier month (188959484).
- Update dates come from Steam's news for the game; only titles and opening text were read.
- The 40-player co-op claim and what carries between runs are reviewers' words, not checked.
- Only English was read; about 4,000 reviews in other languages were not. 1 English-tagged review is in
  Norwegian.
- A cartoon game about crabs; its humour is part of the praise, so tone lessons may not carry to a
  serious sci-fi game.

---

## EARTH DEFENSE FORCE 5

**Read:** 1,760 of 7,208 English reviews (24.5%; 1,768 were read and 8 were not about the game),
±2.58%, written July 2019 to September 2026. **95.7% thumbs up** in the sample (Steam, all languages:
94%, Very Positive).
**Pages:** `findings/earth-defense-force-5-english.md`, `findings/earth-defense-force-5.md`,
`findings/cross-game.md` section 23.

**What it is:** A third-person sci-fi PvE shooter against alien invaders. Up to four players online, or
two on one screen (split screen). One player hosts the game. You play missions in a long, straight
campaign, with four classes, on rising difficulty levels; there are no runs and no extraction. Weapons
and armour drop from enemies and must be picked up by hand. Paid, bought once (the pages do not give the
price; buying on sale is the top complaint). Out on Steam on 2019-07-11. Allied soldiers fight beside you
and sing. The pages do not name the engine or the studio. Its sequel, EDF 6, came to Steam on 2024-07-25.

**How close to Dominion:** Close on the core. It shares the third-person camera, four-player PvE co-op,
alien invaders, and a game hosted by one player, which is Dominion's own setup. It differs in having a
linear mission campaign instead of runs, no extraction, a campy joke tone, and almost no updates or live
service.

### The short version (plain words)
Players love it for being itself: the deliberate cheese (169 times, a tag no other game uses) and the
lines they quote back (134, more than ten times any other game). It is a friends game (146) with four
classes that play like four games (71). The top complaint is not the game but the price: "buy it on sale"
is the most common complaint in every period (60). The design complaints that do sit inside the game are
about the online rules of a game hosted by one player, and picking up loot by hand. None is large alone,
but they are the part of this game closest to Dominion.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (bullets). 787 more thumbs-up reviews said nothing specific.
1. **Cheesy on purpose.** Said 169 times (9.6 per 100). Used by no other game. Players praise what other
   games are marked down for.
2. **Much better with friends.** Said 146 times (8.3 per 100).
3. **Lines players remember and quote.** Said 134 times (7.6 per 100). Every other game of 300 or more
   reviews is at 0.6 or less. The marching song and *"They look just like us!"* are quoted as whole
   reviews.
4. **A worthy sequel to 4.1.** Said 133 times (7.6 per 100). 22.2 per 100 at launch, 3.6 from 2024 on.
5. **Each class plays its own way.** Said 71 times (4.0 per 100). Rose to 5.8 per 100 after 2024-08.
6. **A game you can relax to.** Said 66 times (3.8 per 100).
7. **Plenty of content.** Said 65 times (3.7 per 100).
8. **Builds and gear are deep and varied.** Said 60 times (3.4 per 100).
9. **Keeps pulling you back.** Said 48 times (2.7 per 100).
10. **Destruction changes how you play.** Said 44 times (2.5 per 100), the highest in the corpus (Deep
    Rock Galactic 1.4).
11. **Works well solo.** Said 39 times (2.2 per 100).
12. **Progress feels satisfying.** Said 39 times (2.2 per 100).
13. **The price is fair.** Said 37 times (2.1 per 100).
14. **Good enemy variety.** Said 30 times (1.7 per 100).
15. **The pressure of huge enemy numbers feels good.** Said 24 times (1.4 per 100).
16. **Beats its rivals.** Said 24 times (1.4 per 100). Rose from 0.4 per 100 in 2019-23 to 3.2 after
    2024-08, as Helldivers 2 arrived.
17. **Hard in a good way.** Said 23 times (1.3 per 100).
18. **A real sense of scale.** Said 22 times (1.2 per 100). Only in this game.
19. **Friendly fire makes stories.** Said 21 times (1.2 per 100).
20. **Funny or memorable characters.** Said 21 times (1.2 per 100).
21. **Small conveniences are looked after.** Said 20 times (1.1 per 100).
22. **Best game of its kind.** Said 19 times (1.1 per 100).
23. **Split screen is offered.** Said 18 times (1.0 per 100). Only in this game.
24. **The grind feels earned.** Said 15 times (0.9 per 100).
25. **More of the same is the point.** 13 (only in this game).
26. **The rough edges are part of the charm.** 11 (only in this game). 3 more call it so bad it is good.
27. **Singing, chanting and voice lines are a highlight** (actions with no game effect). 8, all about
    singing or chanting with the allied soldiers. 195194393 (5 helpful) notes the squad picks songs to
    fit the moment: a cave song in caves, a sadder verse once hurt.
28. **No extra account needed.** 5 (built here). One reviewer bought EDF 5 because EDF 6 launched needing
    an Epic account (178414607; reviewer's claim).
29. **Steam Remote Play Together** takes the couch game online: 5.
30. **Allied soldiers pull their weight:** 4.
31. **The online gear cap keeps a mixed group even:** 1 (194931079, *"so everyone can enjoy the game in
    balanced way"*).

The two most-helpful reviews are one-line quips (186 and 180 helpful). The second is a joke that
Helldivers 2 is "EDF at home" (158872972).

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said (bullets). **463 of the 684 complaint bullets (67.7%) come from reviews that
still recommend the game.** Only 76 reviews are thumbs down, with a median of 10 hours played (all
reviews: 30).
1. **Only worth buying on sale.** 60 (3.4 per 100). The top complaint in every period; fourth in the
   corpus after Redfall (17.0), Aliens: Fireteam Elite (9.1) and Back 4 Blood (8.6).
2. **Looks dated.** 44 (2.5 per 100), the highest in the corpus (Redfall 1.9).
3. **Too grindy.** 34 (1.9 per 100).
4. **The price is too high for what it is.** 20 (1.1 per 100).
5. **The sequel changes too little.** 16 (0.9 per 100).
6. **Repetitive.** 16 (0.9 per 100).
7. **Voices and songs weaker than 4.1's.** 16 (0.9 per 100). Built for this game.
8. **Some options are never worth taking.** 12 (0.7 per 100).
9. **4.1 was better** (the sequel claim backfired). 12 (0.7 per 100).
10. **Punishing to play alone.** 12 (0.7 per 100); 8 of the 12 after 2024-08 (1.6 per 100).
11. **One enemy type is hated.** 11 (0.6 per 100).
12. **Vehicles handle badly.** 11 (0.6 per 100). Only in this game.
13. **A class is underpowered.** 11 (0.6 per 100). The Wing Diver is 5 of the 11, often as weaker than in
    4.1; one player finds the other classes dull next to it.
14. **Slow to get going.** 11 (0.6 per 100).
15. **Unstable frame rate.** 10 (0.6 per 100).
16. **Collecting the drops by hand is a chore.** 10 (0.6 per 100). Only in this game. 5 more use a cheat
    and 4 a mod to collect them.
17. **Crashes again and again.** 9 (0.5 per 100).
18. **The thing you need may never drop.** 9 (0.5 per 100).
19. **Voice lines grate or repeat.** 9 (0.5 per 100).
20. **Only repeating content completes the achievements.** 9 (0.5 per 100). 9 reviews say 100% needs
    every mission on the top difficulty with all four classes; 191333532 counts about 580 missions.
21. **Poorly optimised.** 8 (0.5 per 100).
22. **Thumbs down with no reason.** 7 (0.4 per 100).
23. **Movement is sluggish.** 7 (0.4 per 100).
24. **Solo and online progress are separate.** 7 (0.4 per 100).
25. **Difficulty steps badly scaled.** 7 (0.4 per 100).
26. **Too long, padded campaign.** 7. One review says about 40 missions exist only to introduce one new
    enemy (191333532).
27. **Online games cap the gear you bring.** 4.
28. **Cannot join a mission in progress.** 4.
29. **Allied soldiers are no help.** 3.
30. **Each mission caps your weapon level.** 2.
31. **Online scaling is uneven by group size.** 2. *"1 player = 4 player scaling, 2 & 3 = 2 player
    scaling"* (193922521, 6 helpful); the reviewer says this makes solo online far too hard and three
    players far too easy. Reviewer's numbers, not checked.
32. **Drops left on the field are lost when the mission ends.** 2. *"you only get like 10 seconds to
    collect ... after mission complete"* (186836235, 8 helpful).
33. **The host leaving ends the game for everyone.** 1.
34. **Some missions exist only online.** 1.
35. **Split screen cannot go online.** 1. **The second player starts without the first player's armour.**
    1.

### How it changed over time
Four periods. The launch and 2024-01 to 2024-07 periods are small (about ±8), so read for direction.
Helldivers 2 came out in 2024-02 and EDF 6 on Steam in 2024-07.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Launch (July-Dec 2019) | 167 | 96.4% | 115.0 | 43.7 |
| 2020-23 | 955 | 96.6% | 94.3 | 34.1 |
| Helldivers 2 year (Jan-July 2024) | 140 | 97.9% | 86.4 | 25.0 |
| After EDF 6 (Aug 2024 to Sept 2026) | 498 | 93.0% | 93.8 | 48.8 |

- **The launch is loud both ways:** most praise per 100, mostly "better than 4.1"; and a high complaint
  rate, led by dated looks and price.
- **After EDF 6 complaints are the highest of any period.** Playing alone (0.0 at launch → 1.6 per 100),
  repetition (1.6), hated enemies (1.4), voice lines that wear (1.0) and "4.1 was better" (1.2) all
  rise.
- **A new yardstick without the game changing.** Reviews naming Helldivers: 0 (2019-23), 10 (early
  2024), 26 (from 2024-08); 36 in all, none thumbs down. Naming EDF 6: 10, 8, 15 (33 in all). Naming
  4.1: 92, 6, 27 (125 in all, 11 thumbs down). "Beats its rivals" per 100: 0.4, 2.1, 3.2.
- **Helldivers 2 is mostly the foil.** *"Helldivers before Helldivers"* (213662469); the better
  Helldivers 2 (218009571); two use jab names for it. One review says Helldivers 2 does it better and
  still recommends EDF 5: *"Helldivers 2 improved on the formula in pretty much every way but it's still
  a joy"* (206084077).
- **Some came because Helldivers 2 was not sold in their country:** 2 reviews, both 2024-05 (165319991,
  165288608).
- **EDF 6 split fans: 7 put 5 above it, 2 below.** *"6 is basically a worse rehash of 5"* (225531414);
  *"still more stable in multiplayer than EDF6"* (229993356); *"just buy EDF6 instead"* (179277084).
  201902598 (11 helpful) prefers 6 but calls 5 the better first game.
- **Classes grow as a talking point:** each class plays its own way rises to 5.8 per 100 after 2024-08.

### Co-op and online play
- **Friends:** better with friends 146 (8.3 per 100).
- **Hosting:** one player hosts. The host leaving ends the game for everyone (1).
- **Online rules that draw complaints (reviews):** solo and online progress are separate 7; online games
  cap the gear you bring 4; cannot join a mission in progress 4; each mission caps your weapon level 2;
  uneven scaling by group size 2; some missions only online 1.
- **Scaling by head count** is the most detailed complaint (193922521, above).
- **The gear cap is also praised** by one review for keeping a mixed group even (194931079).
- **Couch co-op:** split screen praised 18; but it cannot go online (1), and player two starts without
  player one's armour (1). 5 use Steam Remote Play Together to play the couch mode online.
- **Solo:** works solo 39 against punishing solo 12 (rising to 1.6 per 100 after 2024-08).
- **Bots:** allied soldiers split opinion, 4 say they pull their weight, 3 say they are no help. They
  sing (8 love it).
- **Friendly fire:** makes stories, 21 (praise).
- **No extra account:** 5 praise that no third-party login is needed.
- The pages say nothing about matchmaking with strangers, lag or crossplay.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Four classes, four games:** 71, rising to 5.8 per 100. A class underpowered 11 (Wing Diver 5 of
  them). Some options never worth taking 12.
- **Destruction and scale are the show:** destruction changes play 44; a real sense of scale 22; the
  pressure of huge numbers feels good 24. Players accept plain graphics for hundreds of enemies on
  screen.
- **Vehicles handle badly:** 11.
- **Movement is sluggish:** 7.
- **Friendly fire makes stories:** 21.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Good enemy variety:** 30. **Pressure feels good:** 24.
- **One enemy type is hated:** 11, rising to 1.4 per 100 after EDF 6.
- **Hard in a good way:** 23. **Difficulty steps badly scaled:** 7.
- **Solo gets harder:** punishing solo 12; online scaling makes solo online far too hard (193922521).
- **The end game:** 9 reviews say 100% means every mission on the top difficulty with all four classes
  (about 580 missions, 191333532).

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **The grind is long and mostly accepted:** too grindy 34; grind feels earned 15; progress feels
  satisfying 39; deep and varied gear 60.
- **Loot by hand:** collecting drops is a chore 10; cheats 5 and mods 4 used to collect them; 2 lose
  drops left when a mission ends (about 10 seconds to collect, 186836235).
- **Luck:** the thing you need may never drop 9.
- **Caps:** online gear cap 4; weapon level capped per mission 2.
- **Separate progress:** solo and online 7.
- **Achievements:** only repeating content completes the set 9.

### Runs, content and replay value
- No runs. A long linear campaign of missions, repeated across classes and difficulty levels.
- **Plenty of content:** 65. **Keeps pulling you back:** 48. **A game you can relax to:** 66.
- **Against:** repetitive 16 (rising to 1.6 per 100 after EDF 6); too long and padded 7 (about 40
  missions just to introduce one enemy, 191333532); slow to get going 11.
- **More of the same is the point:** 13.

### Money and price
- **Buy on sale only:** 60, the top complaint in every period.
- **Too expensive for what it is:** 20.
- **Fair price:** 37.
- The pages name no shop, DLC or season-pass complaints.

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- **Engineering is only 3.1% of what players say.**
- **Unstable frame rate** 10; **crashes again and again** 9; **poorly optimised** 8.
- **Looks dated** 44, but players accept it for the enemy counts.
- **The rough edges are part of the charm** 11.
- **EDF 6 in comparison:** one review says 5 is *"still more stable in multiplayer than EDF6"*
  (229993356).

### The studio, updates and community
- **Almost no updates are talked about:** only 3 bullets on updates in 1,760 reviews. The game is bought
  and finished, not run as a service. No patch notes were pulled.
- **The community is a joke culture:** many short reviews quote a line or chant "EDF!". 1,009 of 1,760
  (57%) are 15 words or fewer.
- **Sequel framing:** a worthy sequel to 4.1 (133) against changes too little (16), 4.1 better (12),
  weaker voices (16).
- **Third-party accounts:** EDF 6 launched needing an Epic account, a reviewer says (178414607); 5 praise
  EDF 5 for needing none.

### What players asked for
The pages give few requests as requests; these are what the complaints ask for.
- **Shared progress between solo and online:** 7 (complaint that it is separate).
- **Joining a mission in progress:** 4.
- **No gear cap online:** 4 (but 1 likes it).
- **Even scaling by group size:** 2.
- **Drops collected for you:** 10 call it a chore; 5 cheat and 4 mod to do it; 2 want more time.
- **Split screen online** and **the second player's armour** carried over: 1 each.
- **A lower price:** 60 say wait for a sale; 20 say too expensive.

### Only in this game
67 tags appear in no other game; 35 of them are used once. The ones that matter for design:
- Cheesy on purpose: 169 (praise).
- A real sense of scale: 22 (praise).
- Split screen is offered: 18 (praise).
- Voices weaker than the last game: 16.
- More of the same is the point: 13 (praise).
- The rough edges are part of the charm: 11 (praise).
- Vehicles handle badly: 11.
- Collecting the drops by hand is a chore: 10.
- Also built here: solo and online progress are separate (7), online games cap the gear you bring (4,
  and a praise mode beside it), cannot join a match in progress (4), each mission caps your weapon level
  (2), uneven scaling steps (2), the host leaving ends everyone's run (1), some missions need online play
  (1), no extra account needed (5), came because a rival was not sold here (2), a named rival does it
  better but still recommended.

### For Dominion - our reading
1. **A squad that talks, and a line players can repeat, is worth more than almost any other praise.**
   Evidence: lines players quote 134 (7.6 per 100; every other game 0.6 or less); cheesy on purpose
   169; singing with the allied soldiers 8; songs that fit the moment (195194393). The cross-game page
   says these cost little and are what this game's reviews are about.
   Dominion could: give the squad, the AI handler or the enemies short, quotable lines and maybe a team
   chant; make lines react to the moment (caves, low health, extraction).
   Strength: strong.
2. **But voice lines wear thin if they repeat.**
   Evidence: voice lines grate or repeat 9; voice lines that wear rise to 1.0 per 100 later; voices
   weaker than the last game 16.
   Dominion could: write many variants per moment and limit how often each plays.
   Strength: medium.
3. **Decide early how progress and gear work between solo and online, and say so on the store page.**
   Evidence: solo and online progress separate 7; online gear cap 4; weapon level cap per mission 2;
   some missions only online 1. The cross-game page gives this lesson directly.
   Dominion could: keep one profile for solo and co-op; if gear is capped online, make it a host option,
   not a rule.
   Strength: medium (small counts, but the same hosting model as Dominion).
4. **Let players join a run in progress, and do not end the game when the host leaves.**
   Evidence: cannot join a mission in progress 4; the host leaving ends the game for everyone 1.
   Dominion could: support drop-in joining mid-run; look at host migration or a safe save-and-resume
   when the host quits, since Dominion is hosted by one player too.
   Strength: medium (small counts, direct match to Dominion's setup).
5. **Scale smoothly by the number of players.**
   Evidence: uneven scaling 2; *"1 player = 4 player scaling, 2 & 3 = 2 player scaling"* (193922521),
   which makes solo online too hard and three players too easy (reviewer's numbers); punishing solo 12,
   rising to 1.6 per 100.
   Dominion could: give each group size (1, 2, 3, 4) its own tuning, and retune when someone joins or
   leaves.
   Strength: medium.
6. **A gear cap can be a choice that keeps mixed groups even.**
   Evidence: 1 review praises a room that caps gear to the lowest player (194931079); 4 dislike forced
   caps.
   Dominion could: offer an optional "level the team" setting for mixed groups.
   Strength: weak.
7. **Do not make players pick up loot by hand, and never delete it at the end.**
   Evidence: collecting drops is a chore 10; 5 cheat and 4 mod to collect them; 2 lose drops left on the
   field (about 10 seconds after a mission, 186836235).
   Dominion could: pull loot to players nearby automatically, or credit run loot at extraction; if
   leaving loot behind is part of extraction risk, make that clear.
   Strength: medium.
8. **Classes that feel like separate games are a big draw. Keep them balanced.**
   Evidence: each class plays its own way 71, rising to 5.8 per 100; a class underpowered 11 (Wing
   Diver 5); some options never worth taking 12.
   Dominion could: make each class change how you move and fight, not only numbers; watch that no class
   falls behind.
   Strength: strong.
9. **Hordes, destruction and scale can cover plain graphics.**
   Evidence: destruction changes play 44 (highest in the corpus); a real sense of scale 22; pressure of
   huge numbers 24; good enemy variety 30. Still, looks dated 44.
   Dominion could: favour many enemies and things that break over high-detail models, and budget for it
   on the host's machine.
   Strength: medium.
10. **Price so players do not wait for a sale.**
    Evidence: buy on sale only 60, the top complaint in every period; too expensive 20; fair 37.
    Dominion could: set a launch price players see as fair for what it has, since waiting for a sale is
    the complaint people keep.
    Strength: medium.
11. **Solo must work, and solo becomes more common as new players arrive.**
    Evidence: works solo 39; punishing solo 12, with 8 of the 12 after 2024-08 (from 0.0 to 1.6 per
    100); allied soldiers split 4 helpful against 3 no help.
    Dominion could: tune solo separately, and if AI allies exist, make them useful.
    Strength: medium.
12. **Do not require a third-party account.**
    Evidence: 5 praise needing no extra account; one bought EDF 5 because EDF 6 needed an Epic account
    (178414607).
    Dominion could: if it uses Epic Online Services, let players play with only their Steam account and
    no extra login.
    Strength: medium (small count, very concrete).
13. **Friendly fire can be fun in a PvE co-op game.**
    Evidence: friendly fire makes stories 21.
    Dominion could: consider light friendly fire or knockback as a setting.
    Strength: weak to medium.
14. **Remote play and couch play are wanted by some.**
    Evidence: split screen praised 18; 5 use Steam Remote Play Together.
    Dominion could: make sure Steam Remote Play Together works, even without split screen.
    Strength: weak.
15. **A long grind is accepted if progress is satisfying, but 100% should not need endless repeats.**
    Evidence: grindy 34 against grind feels earned 15 and satisfying progress 39; only repeating
    completes the achievements 9; about 580 missions for 100% (191333532); may never drop 9.
    Dominion could: give achievements new goals, not repeats across every class and difficulty.
    Strength: medium.
16. **Do not pad the start or the length.**
    Evidence: slow to get going 11; too long and padded 7; repetitive 16 (rising later); plenty of content
    65.
    Dominion could: get players into a real fight fast; make each new enemy arrive inside a run, not in
    its own filler mission.
    Strength: medium.
17. **Vehicles, if any, must handle well.**
    Evidence: vehicles handle badly 11.
    Dominion could: skip vehicles or give them the same care as movement.
    Strength: weak to medium.
18. **A new rival changes what reviews are about.**
    Evidence: no review named Helldivers before 2024; 36 do after, none thumbs down; beats its rivals
    0.4 → 3.2 per 100.
    Dominion could: expect to be measured against Helldivers 2 (and EDF), and decide what it does better.
    Strength: medium.
19. **Small conveniences are noticed.**
    Evidence: quality of life looked after 20.
    Dominion could: keep a list of small comforts (auto-loot, quick restart, clear menus).
    Strength: weak.
20. **A game you can relax to is a real draw.**
    Evidence: 66 say they use it to unwind.
    Dominion could: offer an easy setting that still feels like the full game.
    Strength: medium.
21. **A sequel must change enough and keep what was loved.**
    Evidence: worthy sequel 133; changes too little 16; the last game was better 12; voices weaker 16.
    Dominion could: keep for later; it matters only for a Dominion 2.
    Strength: weak (for now).

### Limits
- The sample is 24.5% of English reviews, ±2.58% on whole-sample shares; the two small periods carry
  about ±8.
- The launch month (2019-07) carries the most weight (×14).
- Even at 24.5% read, the online frictions are counts of 1 to 7.
- 102 reviews (5.8%) were edited later and counted on the date they were made.
- No patch notes were pulled. The Helldivers 2 (2024-02-08) and EDF 6 (2024-07-25) dates are Steam's;
  the regional delisting, EDF 6's account requirement, the scaling steps and the drop window are
  reviewers' claims, not checked.
- Many reviews are jokes; a joke was counted under what it praised only when the text said so.
- Only English was read; about 4,300 reviews in other languages were not. 11 English-tagged reviews are
  in other languages.
- Almost no live service, so it says nothing about seasons or updates.

---

## Gunfire Reborn

**Read:** 1,884 of 43,869 English reviews (4.3%), ±2.51%, written 2020-05 to 2026-09. **93.9% thumbs up**
in the sample (1,764 of 1,878; Steam, all languages: 93%, *Very Positive*).
**Pages:** `findings/gunfire-reborn-english.md`, `findings/gunfire-reborn.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 22.

**What it is:** A roguelite shooter played in first person, alone or with up to four players, online.
You play one of a cast of animal heroes. Each run is a string of floors, and your build grows inside the
run. It is a paid game with paid DLC characters and free seasons that stay playable after they end. It
also came out on console. Early Access on Steam from 2020-05; full release 2021-11-17 (Steam's date).
Hosting: three reviewers say the studio moved play sessions off players' own machines and onto its own
servers at some point. The pages do not name the engine, the studio, or the setting beyond the animal
cast. Players sum it up as "Borderlands as a roguelike".

**How close to Dominion:** Close on the loop. It shares four-player PvE co-op, runs that start and end,
builds that grow inside a run, and a live season model. The active loop picked it as the next closest
game to Dominion (it is A18 on the action-roguelike planning list). It differs in the camera (first
person, not third), and it has no extraction and no PvP. Its hosting moved, by the reviewers' account,
from players' machines to the studio's servers, which is the opposite way from Dominion's listen server.

### The short version (plain words)
This is a game people play with friends: "much better with friends" was said 181 times, more than any
specific thing. It also works alone (56 times) far more often than it punishes solo players (14). What
people love is the build that grows during a run (110 times), the pull to play again (106) and a fair
price (100). The top complaint is the English text, which reads badly (36 reviews). Over six years the
thumbs-up share slid from 97.9% to 88.8%, and complaints more than doubled, from 20.9 to 46.3 per 100
reviews, driven by poor translation, slow movement and too few players to match with, not by the core
loop. The newest thumbs-down reviews are mostly about things outside the game: a clause in the user
agreement, AI-made art and a console version left without updates.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (one review can say several things), out of 1,878 reviews.

1. **Much better with friends** - 181 times (9.6 per 100 reviews). Fourth in the whole corpus, behind
   Aliens: Fireteam Elite (10.4), Deep Rock Galactic (10.1) and Remnant II (10.1). Ten reviews say it is
   how they play with family. This praise holds in every period (7.1 to 11.6 per 100).
2. **Builds are deep and varied** - 110 times (5.9 per 100).
3. **It keeps pulling you back** - 106 times (5.6 per 100).
4. **The price is fair** - 100 times (5.3 per 100).
5. **The cast of animal heroes is appealing** - 79 times (4.2 per 100).
6. **Updates keep coming** - 73 times (3.9 per 100), **the highest rate in the corpus** (Warframe 3.6,
   Deep Rock Galactic 3.2).
7. **Combat hits hard** - 69 times (3.7 per 100).
8. **It works alone** - 56 times (3.0 per 100).
9. **Progress feels satisfying** - 55 times (2.9 per 100). *"at the end of every floor you ALWAYS get a
   core character upgrade. The pacing is genuinely the best here that I have ever seen in the genre"*
   (235815154).
10. **It looks well directed** - 50 times (2.7 per 100).
11. **It is hard in a good way** - 48 times (2.6 per 100).
12. **Each hero plays its own way** - 48 times (2.6 per 100).
13. **You get to feel overpowered** - 48 times (2.6 per 100). This rose from 1.1 per 100 in Early Access
    to 4.8 per 100 in 2025-26.
14. **Randomness keeps runs fresh** - 47 times (2.5 per 100).
15. **The best game of its kind** - 46 times (2.4 per 100).
16. **"I want more because it is good"** (more heroes, more DLC) - 42 times (2.2 per 100).
17. **A game you can relax to** - 30 times (1.6 per 100).
18. **A patch made it better** - 29 times (1.5 per 100).
19. **Every weapon or option plays differently** - 24 times (1.3 per 100).
20. **The difficulty levels are well spaced, so you pick your own** - 24 times (1.3 per 100).
21. **The DLC is fair** - 24 times (1.3 per 100).
22. **The developers listen and change things** - 15 times (0.8 per 100).
23. **It does not feel like an unfinished Early Access game** - 15 times (0.8 per 100).
24. **It is made with care** - 15 times (0.8 per 100).
25. **Old seasons never expire** - 6 reviews. Old seasons stay playable and their rewards can be bought
    later with currency earned by playing. *"No FOMO"* (220125902, 545 hours).
26. **Loot is shared fairly** - 4 reviews like that nobody on the team fights over drops. *"You can share
    nearly everything except for cash"* (223278780, 568 hours).
27. **Bot teammates** - said 8 times in 6 reviews. One player called bot teammates *"an absolute
    godsend"* (235865471).
28. **The odd English is charming** - 3 reviews enjoy the strange wording.
29. **Named rivals, in its favour:** 114 reviews name Borderlands (4 of them thumbs down), 57 name Risk of
    Rain (1 down), and 9 name Roboquest (all thumbs up), usually as the closest rival. Explaining the game
    by naming other games was said 147 times, the third most common thing said.

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are reviews, out of 1,878. **387 of the 621 complaints (62.3%) come from reviews that still
recommend the game.** The 114 thumbs-down reviews have a median of 13 hours played; the median for all
reviews is 26. The page does not split each complaint by thumb.

1. **The English reads badly** - 36 reviews (1.9 per 100), the top complaint, plus one review on text
   left in Chinese. That rate is **ten times the next game** of 300 or more reviews in the corpus
   (Escape from Duckov 0.2). It grows with each update: *"the translation for some of the free update
   stuff is pretty shockingly subpar at times, though the most recent season has been much improved"*
   (220774330). Another says *"every single item in the game [is] at least 3 lines long"*, and that the
   fan-made translation packs mostly cover only the base game (230540835).
2. **The start is slow** - 22 reviews (1.2 per 100). This is **the highest rate in the corpus**, just
   ahead of Warframe (1.1). Early runs are weak until the between-run upgrades build up.
3. **Too grindy** - 18 reviews (1.0 per 100).
4. (18 times a thumbs-down review said nothing specific.)
5. **Repetitive** - 17 reviews (0.9 per 100).
6. **Too little content** - 16 reviews (0.9 per 100).
7. **The thing you need may never show up** in the random rolls - 15 reviews (0.8 per 100).
8. **Movement feels slow** - 15 reviews (0.8 per 100).
9. **Too hard alone** - 14 reviews (0.7 per 100).
10. **Lag and out-of-sync play** - 12 reviews (0.6 per 100).
11. **Luck decides the outcome** - 12 reviews (0.6 per 100).
12. **Slow to find a game** with strangers - 11 reviews (0.6 per 100).
13. **One option beats all the others** - 10 reviews (0.5 per 100).
14. **No mod support** - 10 reviews (0.5 per 100).
15. **Enemies kill you in one hit** - 10 reviews (0.5 per 100), at the top difficulty; it rose to 1.2 per
    100 in 2025-26.
16. **Some options are never worth taking** - 9 reviews (0.5 per 100).
17. **Missing modern moves** (no sprint, a short dash) - 9 reviews (0.5 per 100).
18. **Only worth buying on sale** - 8 reviews (0.4 per 100).
19. **Cannot find a game at all** - 8 reviews (0.4 per 100).
20. **Not enough enemy variety** - 8 reviews (0.4 per 100).
21. **Too easy** - 7 reviews (0.4 per 100).
22. **Something is too strong and should be weakened** - 7 reviews (0.4 per 100).
23. **Poorly explained** - 7 reviews (0.4 per 100).
24. **Another game does it better** - 7 reviews (0.4 per 100).
25. **Cannot rejoin a match** - 7 reviews (0.4 per 100).
26. **Too few players, in all** - 23 reviews across slow matchmaking, no matches, "dead game" and "the
    numbers are falling".
27. **Paid heroes** - 10 reviews say DLC heroes are stronger, or that too much of the roster costs extra.
    One thumbs-down review says full completion needs paid DLC (216038763).
28. **Console version left without updates** - 6 reviews.
29. **AI-made art** - 5 reviews, all thumbs down, all from 2026-05 to 2026-09.
30. **A clause in the user agreement** (forced arbitration, the reviewer says) - 1 review, but it is the
    most-helpful thumbs-down review in the sample, with 91 people finding it helpful (207418062).
31. **One playstyle has too few tools** to build around - 3 reviews.
32. **The studio treats its staff badly** (the reviewer says) - 2 reviews.
33. **You cannot choose your starting weapon** - 2 reviews.
34. **Weak-spot hits do not count** as critical hits - 2 reviews.

### How it changed over time
Each period carries about ±4 to ±5 on a share, so read the direction, not the exact figure.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Early Access (2020-05 to 2021-10) | 569 | 97.9% | 105.3 | 20.9 |
| Release and 2022 (2021-11 to 2022-12) | 411 | 94.2% | 91.5 | 28.5 |
| 2023-24 | 479 | 93.5% | 76.6 | 36.1 |
| 2025-26 (to 2026-09) | 419 | 88.8% | 88.5 | 46.3 |

- **Complaints more than doubled while praise stayed between 76.6 and 105.3 per 100.** Three clusters
  carry the rise (counts are reviews per period, in the order above):
  - Translation: 7, 2, 8, **20**. Each new update brings new English, and the late reviews say the new
    season text is the worst.
  - Slow movement and missing moves: 3, 2, 3, **14**. 14 of the 22 reviews on movement are from 2025-26.
  - Too few players: 2, 3, 8, **10**.
- **Feeling overpowered rose** from 1.1 per 100 in Early Access to 4.8 in 2025-26, and one-hit deaths
  rose to 1.2 per 100 in 2025-26.
- **Outside events came late.** The user-agreement clause, AI art (2026-05 to 2026-09) and the console
  version left behind are all 2025-26 events.
- **Events the reviewers tie turns to** (from reviews, not patch notes): bots added in a patch won back a
  player who had given up on solo; reconnecting added in a patch flipped one review to a thumbs up; the
  move to studio servers made lag worse, say 3 reviewers.
- The page's lesson: a game read only in its first year would miss all three late clusters.

### Co-op and online play
- **Friends carry the game.** "Much better with friends" 181 times; 10 reviews play with family.
- **It also works alone.** Works solo 56 times; too hard alone 14.
- **Dropping out and rejoining: 8 reviews** say you cannot rejoin, or a disconnect loses the run. The
  second most helpful thumbs-down review (71 helpful): *"Absolutely disgusting that you can't reconnect
  to your own game session if someone DC's"* (168616519). One review flipped to a thumbs up when patch
  notes said reconnecting had been added (171154399).
- **Joining a run already under way: 3 reviews** ask for it, one naming Deep Rock Galactic as the model
  (179561786, 13 helpful).
- **Hosting: 3 reviews** say lag got worse after the studio moved sessions off players' machines to its
  own servers (173877091, 174405754, 178523682). This is the reviewers' account; no patch notes were
  pulled.
- **Lag and out-of-sync play:** 12 reviews.
- **Loot per player is liked:** 4 reviews say nobody fights over drops; you can share almost everything
  except cash (223278780).
- **Bots:** said 8 times in 6 reviews. One player had written off solo play because solo bosses were
  *"two hit deaths"*, until optional bots came in a patch (192796690). The cost is named too: bots deal
  about an eighth of your damage while enemy health scales up (234049844, the reviewer says).
- **Strangers and matchmaking:** 23 reviews on too few players. *"your game is dying cause of low player
  base"*, with a plea for deep discounts to bring people in (216633728). *"matchmaking worse than tarkov,
  not worth playing if no friends"* (203253379).
- **Friendly fire, crossplay, scaling to the number of players:** the pages say nothing about these.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** (69 times) and **you get to feel overpowered** (48 times, rising over the years).
- **Movement is the one thing about feel that keeps getting worse in the reviews.** Slow movement 15
  reviews and missing modern moves 9, together the most repeated complaint about how the game feels.
  *"You can jump and dash, that is it, not even a sprint"* (230540835). *"you move like a snail with a
  pathetically short dash move"*, with no safety window on the dash (204410221). One reviewer (230540835)
  adds that the slow movement is what keeps fairly simple enemies dangerous.
- **Weak-spot hits that do not count** as critical hits: 2 reviews.
- **Balance:** every option plays differently 24 times; one option beats the rest 10 reviews; some
  options are never worth taking 9; something should be weakened 7; one playstyle has too few tools 3.
- **Each hero plays its own way** - 48 times.
- **Camera:** first person, so the pages say nothing about a third-person camera.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Hard in a good way** 48 times; **difficulty levels well spaced** 24 times.
- **One-hit deaths at the top difficulty:** 10 reviews, rising to 1.2 per 100 in 2025-26.
- **Too easy:** 7 reviews. Players also say their power outgrows the challenge late in a run, so late
  play stops needing thought (no count given).
- **Not enough enemy variety:** 8 reviews.
- **Solo bosses** were *"two hit deaths"* for one player until bots came (192796690).
- The pages say little else about bosses or enemy behaviour.

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Inside a run:** builds deep and varied 110 times; a guaranteed hero upgrade at the end of every floor
  is praised as the best pacing in the genre (235815154); satisfying progress 55 times.
- **Between runs:** the slow start is 22 reviews (highest in the corpus) and grindy 18. The page notes
  that the between-run upgrades that make early runs weak are the same ones players praise later.
- **Randomness:** keeps runs fresh 47 times; the thing you need may never show up 15 reviews; luck decides
  the outcome 12 reviews.
- **Choice at the start:** 2 reviews want to choose their starting weapon.
- **Seasons:** 6 reviews praise that old seasons never expire and their rewards can be bought later with
  earned currency - *"No FOMO"* (220125902).
- **Paid heroes:** 24 call the DLC fair; 10 reviews say paid heroes are stronger or too much is paid.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **It keeps pulling you back** - 106 times. **"I want more because it is good"** - 42 times.
- **A game to relax to** - 30 times.
- **Repetitive** - 17 reviews. **Too little content** - 16 reviews.
- **No mod support** - 10 reviews.
- The pages do not give run length or count levels.

### Money and price
- **The price is fair** - 100 times (5.3 per 100).
- **The DLC is fair** - 24 times.
- **Only worth buying on sale** - 8 reviews.
- **Paid heroes:** 10 reviews say DLC heroes are stronger or too much of the roster costs extra; 1 says
  full completion needs paid DLC (216038763).
- **Seasons:** old season rewards can be bought later with currency earned by playing (6 reviews praise
  it).
- One reviewer asks for deep discounts to bring players back (216633728).

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Engineering is only 3.8% of everything said (128 times).
- **Lag and out-of-sync play:** 12 reviews. **Worse lag after the move to studio servers:** 3 reviews.
- **Cannot rejoin / a disconnect loses the run:** 8 reviews.
- The pages name no crash, save-loss or performance problem in any number. The engine is not named.

### The studio, updates and community
- **Updates keep coming:** 73 times, the highest rate in the corpus. **A patch made it better:** 29.
  **The developers listen and change things:** 15.
- **Each update brings new badly translated text** (translation complaints 7 in Early Access, 20 in
  2025-26).
- **A console version left without updates:** 6 reviews.
- **AI-made art:** 5 thumbs-down reviews, all 2026-05 to 2026-09. The only other game in the corpus where
  this came up is ARC Raiders, with 1.
- **A user-agreement clause:** 1 review, 91 helpful, the most helpful thumbs-down review in the sample
  (207418062).
- **The studio treats its staff badly** (the reviewer says): 2 reviews.
- **Community:** 181 of the 246 community remarks are about playing with friends. Story is almost never
  mentioned (9 times in all); nobody comes for a story. Sound is 17 times; accessibility 3.

### What players asked for
- **Better English text:** 36 reviews on bad wording, 1 on text left in Chinese.
- **Reconnect after a drop:** 8 reviews (added later, a reviewer says).
- **Join a run in progress:** 3 reviews, one naming Deep Rock Galactic.
- **Faster movement or modern moves** (sprint, a longer dash, a dash with a safety window): 15 + 9 reviews
  on movement.
- **Mod support:** 10 reviews.
- **More content:** too little 16 reviews; "I want more because it is good" 42 times.
- **More enemy variety:** 8 reviews.
- **More players / easier matchmaking:** 23 reviews; one asks for deep discounts to get there.
- **Console updates:** 6 reviews.
- **Choose the starting weapon:** 2 reviews.
- **More tools for one playstyle:** 3 reviews.
- **Bots for solo** (asked for and then added, say reviewers): said 8 times in 6 reviews.

### Only in this game
- **22 of the 357 kinds of remark used here appear in no other game**; 26 new kinds were built while
  reading it, and 11 of the 22 were seen only once. The most used:
  - A console version left behind while the PC version is updated - 6 reviews.
  - The odd translated wording is charming - 3.
  - One playstyle has too few tools to build around - 3.
  - You cannot choose your starting weapon - 2.
  - Weak-spot hits do not count as critical hits - 2.
  - The studio treats its staff badly - 2.
- **Translation as a top complaint** - ten times the rate of any other game read at size.
- **The highest rate in the corpus** for steady updates (3.9 per 100) and for the slow start (1.2 per 100).
- **Very short reviews:** mean 32.5 words, and 1,132 of 1,878 (60%) are 15 words or fewer, which is why
  so many remarks are a bare thumbs up (728 times).

### For Dominion - our reading
1. **Any upgrade text players read in the middle of a run must be checked at every update, not once.**
   Evidence: bad English is the top complaint, 36 reviews (1.9 per 100, ten times any other game read at
   size), and it grew from 7 reviews in Early Access to 20 in 2025-26 as each season added new text.
   One reviewer adds that item text runs *"at least 3 lines long"* (230540835). The cross-game page draws this same lesson.
   Dominion could: keep upgrade text short (one line where possible), use icons and numbers, and make a
   text check part of every patch, in every language it ships. Strength: **medium** (clear and rising,
   but tied to this game's translation).
2. **Build for friends first, and make solo work too.** Evidence: "much better with friends" 181 times
   (fourth highest in the corpus); works alone 56 times against too hard alone 14. Dominion could: make
   the four-player session easy to start with friends, and tune and test a real solo path (with scaling
   or bots). Strength: **strong**.
3. **Let a player who drops get back into the run.** Evidence: 8 reviews; the second most helpful
   thumbs-down review (71 helpful) is about not being able to reconnect (168616519); one review turned
   to a thumbs up when reconnecting was added (171154399). Dominion could: on a listen server, let a
   dropped client rejoin the same run with their build kept, and decide what happens when the host drops
   (keep the run alive, save it, or hand it over). Strength: **medium**.
4. **Allow joining a run in progress.** Evidence: 3 reviews, one naming Deep Rock Galactic (179561786, 13
   helpful). Dominion could: let friends drop into a running session, at least between rooms or arenas.
   Strength: **weak**.
5. **Hosting changes are felt.** Evidence: 3 reviews say lag got worse after the move to studio servers;
   lag and out-of-sync play 12 reviews. Dominion could: since it hosts on a player's machine, test play
   on weak home connections and show the host's connection quality before a run. Strength: **weak**.
6. **Movement needs a sprint and a real dodge, or players will feel slow.** Evidence: slow movement 15
   and missing modern moves 9; 14 of the 22 reviews on movement are from 2025-26; *"not even a sprint"*
   (230540835); a dash with no safety window (204410221). The counterpoint, from one reviewer: slow
   movement is what keeps simple enemies dangerous. Dominion could: give a sprint and a dodge with a
   short safety window, and keep enemies threatening by how they attack, not by keeping the player slow.
   Strength: **medium**.
7. **A guaranteed upgrade at fixed points keeps a run building.** Evidence: builds deep and varied 110;
   satisfying progress 55; an upgrade at the end of every floor called the best pacing in the genre
   (235815154). Dominion could: give a sure upgrade after each arena or area, on top of random drops.
   Strength: **strong**.
8. **Let players feel overpowered, but guard the top difficulty against one-shot deaths and boredom.**
   Evidence: feeling overpowered 48 times, rising from 1.1 to 4.8 per 100; one-hit deaths 10 reviews,
   rising to 1.2 per 100 late; too easy 7; power outgrowing the challenge also named. Dominion could: let
   builds get strong, raise challenge with enemy count and behaviour rather than one-shot damage, and
   flag one-shot attacks clearly. Strength: **medium**.
9. **Do not make the first runs feel weak because of between-run upgrades.** Evidence: slow start 22
   reviews (highest in the corpus) and grindy 18. Dominion could: make the first runs fun on their own,
   with meta upgrades adding options more than raw power. Strength: **medium**.
10. **Randomness is liked until the needed piece never comes.** Evidence: keeps runs fresh 47 times;
    the thing you need never shows up 15 reviews; luck decides the outcome 12 reviews. Dominion could:
    add bad-luck protection or a way to steer rolls (rerolls, a shop, a choice of three). Strength:
    **medium**.
11. **Bots can save solo play, but only if their damage keeps up.** Evidence: said 8 times in 6 reviews;
    "an absolute godsend" (235865471); won back a player who found solo bosses too deadly (192796690);
    but bots deal about an eighth of your damage while enemy health scales (234049844). Dominion could:
    if it adds bots, scale enemies to the humans, not the bots. Strength: **weak**.
12. **Loot each player can keep avoids fights inside the team.** Evidence: 4 reviews praise that nobody
    fights over drops (223278780). Dominion could: give each player their own drops, or share them in a
    clear way. Strength: **weak**.
13. **A small player base kills matchmaking with strangers.** Evidence: 23 reviews on too few players,
    rising from 2 to 10 per period. Dominion could: lean on friend invites and solo play, and not build
    features that need a full public lobby to work. Strength: **medium**.
14. **Seasons that never expire are praised.** Evidence: 6 reviews; *"No FOMO"* (220125902, 545 hours).
    Dominion could: if it runs seasons, keep old ones playable and their rewards earnable later.
    Strength: **weak** (few reviews, but no pushback at all).
15. **Paid heroes that are stronger cost trust.** Evidence: DLC fair 24 times, but 10 reviews say paid
    heroes are stronger or too much is paid; 1 complains that full completion needs paid DLC. Dominion
    could: keep paid content side-grades, not upgrades, and keep achievements reachable in the base game.
    Strength: **weak to medium**.
16. **Trust can be lost outside the game.** Evidence: a user-agreement clause (1 review, 91 helpful, the
    top thumbs-down review); AI-made art (5 thumbs-down reviews, 2026-05 to 2026-09); a console version
    left behind (6 reviews). Dominion could: keep the user agreement plain, say how art is made, and keep
    every platform it ships on updated together. Strength: **medium** (small counts, but high in
    helpful votes and all recent).
17. **A steady stream of updates is the strongest praise for a live game.** Evidence: 73 times, the
    highest rate in the corpus; a patch made it better 29; developers listen 15. Dominion could: plan a
    regular update rhythm before launch. Strength: **strong**.
18. **Watch the complaints over years, not just at launch.** Evidence: complaints went from 20.9 to 46.3
    per 100 and the thumb from 97.9% to 88.8% over six years, while praise held. Dominion could: track
    review complaints by period after launch, so a slow slide shows up early. Strength: **medium**.
19. **A one-line pitch that names other games helps players explain it.** Evidence: explained by naming
    other games 147 times; 114 name Borderlands, 57 Risk of Rain; "Borderlands as a roguelike". Dominion
    could: pick its own "X meets Y" line and use it in marketing. Strength: **medium**.
20. **A memorable cast helps.** Evidence: appealing heroes 79 times; each plays its own way 48. Dominion
    could: make each playable character look and play clearly different. Strength: **medium**.
21. **Weak-spot hits must count.** Evidence: 2 reviews say head or weak-spot hits do not register as
    critical. Dominion could: test weak-spot hit boxes against the art. Strength: **weak**.
22. **Story is not why people play this kind of game.** Evidence: story is mentioned 9 times in 3,350
    remarks. Dominion could: keep story light inside runs and spend effort on the loop. Strength: **weak**
    (absence of talk, not a complaint).
23. **Mod support is asked for.** Evidence: 10 reviews. Dominion could: decide early whether to allow
    mods. Strength: **weak**.

### Limits
- A 4.3% sample; whole-sample shares carry ±2.51%, and each of the four periods about ±4 to ±5.
- The first month (2020-05) carries the heaviest weight (×45), because Steam returned only 10 of the 20
  reviews wanted.
- 154 reviews (8.2%) were edited later and are counted on the day they were written; how to treat edits
  is open with Rico.
- No patch notes were pulled. The server move, the reconnect fix, the bots patch, the user-agreement
  clause, the AI art and the console claims all come from reviews and were not checked.
- Hours are those shown on the review.
- Early batches and later batches were read in different sessions; the page cannot rule out that the
  late reader named movement and population complaints more often.
- Reviews are very short (60% are 15 words or fewer); 773 remarks (23.1%) said nothing specific.
- 24 reviews were written in another language. About 60,000 reviews in other languages were not read.

---

## Escape from Duckov

**Read:** 1,166 of 11,004 English reviews (10.6%), ±2.83%, written 2025-10 to 2026-09. **90.9% thumbs up**
in the sample (1,055 of 1,160; Steam, all languages: 84%, *Very Positive*).
**Pages:** `findings/escape-from-duckov-english.md`, `findings/escape-from-duckov.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 21.

**What it is:** A single-player extraction shooter seen from above (top-down), against the game only, no
other players. It is a comic take on Escape from Tarkov, with ducks. You go out on a run, gather loot,
and must get out alive; if you die you lose your gear, with one chance to go back and pick it up. Areas
the reviews name include Farm Town and the Labs (late game). Difficulty can be set in detail, including
whether you lose all your gear on death. There is a character creator and a Steam Workshop for mods.
Paid game. On Steam since 2025-10. The reviews name the studio as Team Soda and the publisher as Bilibili
(not checked against store data). The pages do not name the engine. There is no co-op except through
mods.

**How close to Dominion:** The closest game on either planning list (A1 on the action-roguelike list),
picked under Rico's standing order of 2026-09-25. It shares PvE extraction, runs that start and end,
and loot you can lose. It does not share co-op (it is single-player), the camera (top-down, not third
person), hosting (there is nothing to host), or the sci-fi arena side. So it tells Dominion a lot about
the extraction loop and nothing about playing it together.

### The short version (plain words)
Players judge it against Tarkov, and it wins: 135 of 1,160 reviews name Tarkov, and only 6 of those are
thumbs down. Taking PvP out is read as the feature, not a gap: 51 times, all under a thumbs up, and 16
people who normally avoid the genre say it won them over. Its biggest complaints are wishes from fans:
co-op (39 times, 36 of them thumbs up) and controller support (33 times, and 12 of those 33 are thumbs
down). The thumbs-up share fell from 96.7% to 74.4% in one month (2025-12), and that drop came from a
fight over a workshop mod, not the game. Where the game itself loses people is the end game, where the
part you need is left to chance.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (one review can say several things), out of 1,160 reviews.

1. **It keeps pulling you back** - 98 times (8.4 per 100 reviews).
2. **It beats its rivals** (mostly Tarkov) - 71 times (6.1 per 100), **the highest rate in the corpus**
   (The Anacrusis 5.6, Warframe 2.1). 135 of 1,160 reviews name Tarkov; 6 of them are thumbs down.
   *"Very fun game that takes out the worst part of Tarkov, which is the cheaters"* (216039510).
   Explaining it by naming other games was said 69 more times.
3. **The price is fair** - 68 times (5.9 per 100).
4. **Progress feels satisfying** - 61 times (5.3 per 100).
5. **No PvP is a feature** - 51 times (4.4 per 100), **every one under a thumbs up.** No other game in the
   corpus reaches 1 per 100 on this (Terminull Brigade 0.8; ARC Raiders 0). *"i have never played an
   extraction shooter, mainly because i just dont enjoy PVP."* (211985235, 100 hours).
6. **Hard in a good way** - 49 times (4.2 per 100).
7. **A game you can relax to** - 48 times (4.1 per 100). The same players often say both 6 and 7: the
   difficulty is chosen.
8. **Plenty of content** - 48 times (4.1 per 100).
9. **Looks like a joke, plays like a real game** - 39 times (3.4 per 100), all thumbs up, and said of no
   other game. *"Don't let the silly presentation fool you into thinking this is a joke game."*
   (212777542). A curator review with 127 helpful votes ends *"Just because it's with ducks, don't
   expect it to be any more forgiving!"* (214029794).
10. **Mods extend the game** - 36 times (3.1 per 100).
11. **The stakes are worth the risk** (losing gear makes runs matter) - 33 times (2.8 per 100). *"Loot runs
    are stressful in a good way, deaths hurt, and progression feels earned."* (215425450).
12. **The satire lands** - 31 times (2.7 per 100).
13. **Difficulty levels are well spaced, so you pick your own** - 29 times (2.5 per 100).
14. **Combat hits hard** - 27 times (2.3 per 100).
15. **Made with care** - 21 times (1.8 per 100).
16. **It looks well directed** - 21 times (1.8 per 100).
17. **"I want more because it is good"** - 19 times (1.6 per 100).
18. **Builds are deep and varied** - 19 times (1.6 per 100).
19. **Good in short sittings** - 18 times (1.6 per 100).
