20. **The character creator is fun** - 16 times (1.4 per 100), said of no other game.
21. **It won over someone who avoids the genre** - 16 times (1.4 per 100), all thumbs up; second in the
    corpus only to Remnant II (1.5).
22. **It works alone** - 15 times (1.3 per 100).
23. **Best game of its kind** - 15 times (1.3 per 100).
24. **The music fits** - 13 times (1.1 per 100).
25. **Dying costs little and you get back into it fast** - 8 times.
26. **It runs well** - 7 times; **bugs are rare and minor** - 6 times.
27. **Difficulty can be tuned in detail** - 6 times, said of no other game.

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said, out of 1,160 reviews. **255 of the 469 complaints (54.4%) come from reviews
that still recommend the game.** The 105 thumbs-down reviews have a median of 9 hours played; the median
for all reviews is 19. **Every complaint is small: the top one is 3.4 per 100.**

1. **A mode players expected is missing** - 39 times (3.4 per 100). **36 of the 39 are thumbs up.** 6
   mention PvP; the rest ask for co-op. *"Now if only it had co-op it would be perfect."* (212024443).
2. **No controller support** - 33 times (2.8 per 100). **12 of the 33 are thumbs down**, so this one
   costs votes. 9 of the 33 mention the Steam Deck. A workshop mod adds it, and some reviews point to it.
   *"not having official controller support at this point is criminal"* (228527236).
3. **The part you need may never drop** - 23 times in 21 reviews (2.0 per 100), 4 of the 21 thumbs down.
   **The highest rate in the corpus** (Remnant II 1.7, Warframe 1.2, Risk of Rain 2 0.3).
4. **Managing the inventory is a chore** - 18 times (1.6 per 100).
5. (17 times a thumbs-down review said nothing specific.)
6. **The studio went easy on a mod author who did harm** (the reviewers say) - 16 times (1.4 per 100),
   said of no other game. See the mod dispute below.
7. **The late game turns into a grind** - 14 reviews (1.2 per 100), 4 thumbs down; said of no other game.
8. **Too grindy** - 13 reviews (1.1 per 100), 6 thumbs down.
9. **Quests force how you play** (a named weapon or method) - 9 times (0.8 per 100).
10. **Missing basic conveniences** - 9 times (0.8 per 100).
11. **Repetitive** - 8 times (0.7 per 100).
12. **Mods said to carry harmful code** - 8 times (0.7 per 100).
13. **Dying costs too much** - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
14. **It takes over your life** (a warning) - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
15. **The game hides information you need** - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
16. **A thumbs-down given as a protest**, not about the game - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
17. **The narrow view is a handicap** - 6 times (0.5 per 100).
18. **Most mods are in another language** - 6 times (0.5 per 100).
19. **The studio leans on mods to fill gaps** - 6 times (0.5 per 100).
20. **The praise is undeserved** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
21. **One part is far harder than the rest** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
22. **Each death makes the next try harder** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
23. **Progress is held back by real-world timers** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
24. **Only one chance to get your gear back** - 5 reviews (0.4 per 100), 2 thumbs down.
25. **The line-of-sight view hides enemies** - 4 times (0.3 per 100).
26. **Stutter** - 4 times; few, but severe: one thumbs-down review says it stutters in every Farm Town
    boss fight (232131285).
27. **Too easy** - 2; **too hard** - 2.
28. **The difficulty dial is not fine enough** - 1 review: full gear loss only comes with higher enemy
    damage (214751423).
29. **The duck theme wore thin** - 1 review (217753377).
30. **A collaboration with Tarkov's makers** - 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31.

### How it changed over time
The later periods hold 250 reviews between them and carry about ±8.5 to ±8.8 each; read them for
direction.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 | Mean words per review |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2025-10 (launch) | 294 | 93.2% | 105.4 | 26.5 | 32 |
| 2025-11 | 483 | 96.7% | 96.3 | 22.2 | 29 |
| 2025-12 | 133 | **74.4%** | 102.3 | **91.0** | **71** |
| 2026-01 to 03 | 127 | 83.5% | 103.1 | 63.8 | 52 |
| 2026-04 to 09 | 123 | 88.6% | 70.7 | 43.9 | 33 |

- **2025-12 is the break.** Complaints per 100 quadrupled and reviews more than doubled in length. Part
  of it is the mod dispute (13 of December's 34 thumbs down are about it; the "went easy on the mod
  author" complaint hit 9.0 per 100 that month). Part of it is players reaching the end game.
- **End-game complaints start at zero in the launch month and rise after.** The part you need never
  drops: 0 per 100 in 2025-10, 1.7 in 2025-11, 3.0 in 2025-12, 7.1 in 2026-01 to 03. The late-game grind
  and plain grind are both 0 per 100 at launch and between 1.6 and 3.3 per 100 from 2025-12 on. Players who reach the end write later.
- **Dying costs too much** rises to 2.4 per 100 in 2026-04 to 09 (from 0.0 to 0.7 before).
- **Praise that fell:** plenty of content 6.5 per 100 at launch, 1.6 to 2.4 later; fair price 8.2 to 2.4;
  hard in a good way 4.8 to 1.6; looks well directed 4.4 at launch, under 2.5 after.
- **Praise that rose:** well-spaced difficulty 0.3 at launch to 4.7 in 2026-01 to 03; the satire landing
  peaked at 6.8 in 2025-12.
- **The thumb recovered most of the way** by mid-2026 (88.6%). Copies of one protest statement kept
  appearing until 2026-07.

### Co-op and online play
- **It is single-player.** There is no co-op except through mods, so the pages cannot say anything about
  co-op play itself.
- **Co-op is the top wish.** A missing mode is the top complaint, 39 times, 36 of them thumbs up, and
  most ask for co-op (6 mention PvP).
- **Taking PvP out is praised** (51 times, all thumbs up), and **16 genre-avoiders were won over.**
- **What solo costs:** with nobody to trade with, a missing rare part can only be found by running the
  same route again. The page says this makes the random drop harder to accept.
- **The two extraction games ask for each other** (cross-game): in ARC Raiders (PvP plus PvE), 42 of 55
  missing-mode requests ask for PvE; here, most ask for co-op - more players, on the same side.
- Hosting, joining, dropping out, scaling, bots, friendly fire, matchmaking, crossplay, servers: not
  applicable; the game has none.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** - 27 times (falling from 3.1 per 100 at launch to 0.8 later).
- **Controller support is missing** - 33 times, 12 of them thumbs down; 9 mention the Steam Deck.
- **Camera and sight:** the narrow view is a handicap (6 times); the game draws only what your character
  can see, and 4 complain that this hides enemies.
- **Enemy ammo is too random to prepare for** - from one end-game review (212801415).
- The pages say nothing specific about movement.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Difficulty is a dial, and the dial is praised.** Hard in a good way 49; a game to relax to 48;
  well-spaced levels 29; tuned in detail 6. Too easy and too hard are 2 each. Players describe choosing
  between a relaxed loot run and a punishing one, and some turn full gear loss on or off (212777542).
- **The dial is not fine enough** for one player: full gear loss only comes with higher enemy damage
  (214751423).
- **One part is far harder than the rest** - 5 times. **Each death makes the next try harder** (a
  stronger enemy appears) - 5 times.
- **An area needs gear it never tells you about** - from one end-game review (212801415).
- **Boss fights:** one thumbs-down review says it stutters in every Farm Town boss fight (232131285).
- A curator: *"Just because it's with ducks, don't expect it to be any more forgiving!"* (214029794, 127
  helpful).

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Progress feels satisfying** - 61 times. **Builds deep and varied** - 19. **Character creator fun** - 16.
- **The end game is where it sours.** Five complaints describe the same stretch:

  | What players said | Times | Reviews | Thumbs down |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | The late game turns into a grind | 14 | 14 | 4 |
  | Too grindy | 13 | 13 | 6 |
  | The part you need may never drop | 23 | 21 | 4 |
  | One part is far harder than the rest | 5 | - | - |
  | Only one chance to get your gear back | 5 | 5 | 2 |

  The shape is the same: early and middle game praised, then the last area (the Labs, or Farm Town
  before them), where keys and rare parts must be found by chance. *"once you get to Jlabs, it becomes
  considerably less fun and more of a grind"* (212074993, 98 hours, thumbs up). *"Early to mid game is
  fantastic!"* - then the end game (212801415, 94 hours, thumbs down). *"Duckov absolutely tanks the
  endgame"* (217761449, 84 hours, thumbs down, 12 helpful). A 100%-completion review at 171 hours calls
  some things *"a MASSIVE grind for little reward"* (212775495).
- **The complaint is about chance, not length.**
- **Losing gear:** stakes worth the risk 33; quick recovery keeps the flow 8. Against: only one chance to
  recover your gear 5; dying costs too much 7; each death makes the next try harder 5. Players like that
  dying costs something and resent losing everything twice.
- **Real-world timers** hold progress back - 5 times.
- **Quests force a set weapon or method** - 9 times.
- **Inventory management is a chore** - 18 times; **missing basic conveniences** - 9; **the game hides
  information you need** - 7.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **It keeps pulling you back** - 98 times. **Plenty of content** - 48 times (strongest at launch).
- **Good in short sittings** - 18 times. **A game to relax to** - 48 times.
- **"I want more because it is good"** - 19 times.
- **Repetitive** - 8 times. **Takes over your life** (a warning) - 7.
- **Mods extend the game** - 36 times; but 6 say the studio leans on mods to fill gaps, and 6 that most
  mods are in another language.
- The pages do not give run length.

### Money and price
- **The price is fair** - 68 times (5.9 per 100), falling from 8.2 per 100 at launch to 2.4 in 2026-04
  to 09.
- A collaboration with Tarkov's makers drew 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31 (the reviewers say).
- The pages mention no DLC, shop or season pass.

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Engineering is only 2.4% of everything said (53 times). **Runs well** 7, **bugs rare and minor** 6,
  against **stutter** 4.
- Thumbs-down performance reviews are few but severe: a stutter in every Farm Town boss fight
  (232131285).
- **The one tech gap people leave over is controller support** (33 times, 12 thumbs down, 9 Steam Deck).
- One reviewer says their offline play is not counted in their Steam hours.
- The engine is not named.

### The studio, updates and community
- **Updates and live service:** only 6 remarks in all - the game is under a year old.
- **The mod dispute** (all claims from reviews, not a checked incident): 28 reviews take part, 17 of them
  thumbs down, 13 of those 17 written in 2025-12. The reviews say a popular workshop mod hid code that
  closed the game for players on its author's blacklist, and that the studio asked the author to remove
  it rather than punishing them.
  - The studio went easy on a harmful mod author - 16 times.
  - Mods said to carry harmful code - 8 times.
  - A thumbs-down given as a protest - 7 times.
  - "The controversy did not change my verdict" - 8 times.
  - "The negative reviews are a review bomb" - 2 times.
  - **10 of the 28 defend the game** or tell readers to ignore it, mostly players who never used mods:
    *"I don't know what the downvotes are about. I play the vanilla game, and I enjoy it."* (215970764,
    40 helpful). The dispute review with the most hours is a protest at 233 hours: *"the publisher
    allowing a malicious mod to remain hosted on Steam despite multiple reports is unacceptable."*
    (213339351, 17 helpful).
  - The same statement was pasted at least four times (213987337, 217133568, 226071171, 231744444),
    from 2025-12 to 2026-07.
- **A second, smaller dispute:** a collaboration with Tarkov's makers, 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31.
- **The tone and satire carry the marketing:** looks like a joke, plays like a real game 39; the satire
  lands 31; the character creator 16.

### What players asked for
- **Co-op** - most of the 39 missing-mode requests (6 of the 39 mention PvP).
- **Controller support** - 33 times (9 mention the Steam Deck).
- **Better inventory management** - 18 times; **basic conveniences** - 9; **show the information needed**
  - 7.
- **Less chance in the end game** - the part you need never drops, 23 times; late-game grind 14.
- **More than one chance to get gear back** - 5 reviews; less harsh restarts - 7.
- **Quests that let you play your own way** - 9.
- **A wider view** - 6; **the line-of-sight view to show more** - 4.
- **A finer difficulty dial** (gear loss separate from enemy damage) - 1 review (214751423).
- **Mods in English** - 6.
- **Firmer action against harmful mod authors** - 16.
- **More content** - 19 times as praise ("I want more").

### Only in this game
- **51 of the 289 kinds of remark used here appear in no other game**; 49 new kinds were built while
  reading it, and 28 of the 51 were seen only once. The most used:
  - Looks like a joke, plays like a real game - 39.
  - The character creator is fun - 16.
  - The studio went easy on a harmful mod author - 16.
  - The late game turns into a grind - 14.
  - Quests force how you play - 9.
  - Mods said to carry harmful code - 8.
  - Difficulty can be tuned in detail - 6.
  - Most mods are in another language - 6.
  - Only one chance to get your gear back - 5.
- **Highest rates in the corpus:** no PvP praised as a feature (4.4 per 100; no other game reaches 1),
  beats its rivals (6.1 per 100), the part you need may never drop (2.0 per 100).
- **The first single-player PvE extraction game in the corpus.**

### For Dominion - our reading
The findings page's own "For Dominion" line: this is the nearest game in the queue so far. What carries
over: taking PvP out is praised as a feature and wins over people who avoid the genre; a difficulty dial
lets one game be both relaxing and hard; the corpse run is liked until it becomes one-try-only; the end
game must not rest on a random drop; and co-op is the first thing fans ask for. The lessons below expand
on each.

1. **PvE-only extraction is a selling point, not a gap - say so loudly.** Evidence: no PvP praised as a
   feature 51 times, all thumbs up (no other game reaches 1 per 100); 16 people who avoid the genre were
   won over, all thumbs up; *"i have never played an extraction shooter, mainly because i just dont enjoy
   PVP."* (211985235). Dominion could: put "no PvP, no cheaters" in its pitch, and aim at players
   who like extraction tension but not other players. Strength: **strong**.
2. **Co-op is the first thing fans of PvE extraction want - Dominion's core is the answer.** Evidence:
   the top complaint, 39 times, 36 of them thumbs up, most asking for co-op; in ARC Raiders, 42 of 55
   missing-mode requests ask for PvE (cross-game). Dominion could: treat four-player co-op extraction as
   the headline, and still keep solo working well, since 15 praise this game as a solo game. Strength:
   **strong**.
3. **A difficulty dial lets one game be both relaxing and hard.** Evidence: hard in a good way 49, a game
   to relax to 48, well-spaced levels 29, tuned in detail 6; too easy and too hard only 2 each; some
   players turn full gear loss on or off (212777542). But one player wanted gear loss separate from enemy
   damage (214751423). Dominion could: offer separate settings for enemy strength and for how much you
   lose on death, chosen by the host for the group. Strength: **strong**.
4. **Losing gear is liked when you can get it back; losing it twice is not.** Evidence: stakes worth the
   risk 33; quick recovery keeps the flow 8; *"Loot runs are stressful in a good way, deaths hurt, and
   progression feels earned."* (215425450). Against: only one chance to recover gear 5, dying costs too
   much 7 (rising late to 2.4 per 100), each death makes the next try harder 5. Dominion could: give a
   recovery run, and think about letting teammates recover a fallen player's gear, so the loss is shared
   and not total. Strength: **medium**.
5. **The end game must not rest on a random drop.** Evidence: the part you need may never drop 23 times
   in 21 reviews (the highest rate in the corpus), rising to 7.1 per 100 in 2026-01 to 03; late-game
   grind 14; grindy 13; *"Duckov absolutely tanks the endgame"* (217761449, 12 helpful). The complaint is
   about chance, not length; solo play makes it worse because nobody can trade the part. Dominion
   could: add a sure path to key items (crafting from parts, a bad-luck counter, or trading between
   teammates in a run). Strength: **medium to strong**.
6. **Ship controller support at launch.** Evidence: 33 times, and 12 of the 33 are thumbs down - the one
   tech gap people leave over; 9 mention the Steam Deck. Dominion could: support controllers and the
   Steam Deck from day one, with full rebinding. Strength: **strong**.
7. **Inventory and menus must not be a chore.** Evidence: inventory is a chore 18; missing basic
   conveniences 9; the game hides information you need 7. Dominion could: sort, stack and auto-move loot
   with one button, and show item values and needs clearly at the extraction screen. Strength: **medium**.
8. **Tell players what gear an area needs, and avoid one sudden spike.** Evidence: one part far harder
   than the rest 5; an area that needs gear it never mentions (212801415). Dominion could: show threat and
   gear advice before a run starts. Strength: **weak**.
9. **Do not force a set weapon in quests.** Evidence: quests force how you play 9. Dominion could: let
   goals be met in more than one way. Strength: **weak**.
10. **Real-world timers annoy.** Evidence: 5 times. Dominion could: avoid crafting timers. Strength:
    **weak**.
11. **An outside fight can move the thumb more than the game does - have a mod and moderation policy
    first.** Evidence: thumbs up fell from 96.7% to 74.4% in 2025-12 over a mod dispute; 28 reviews took
    part, 17 thumbs down; the complaint was how the studio handled the mod author (16 times), plus claims
    of harmful code in mods (8). Mods were also praised (36 times). Dominion could: if it opens mod
    support, publish clear rules first, act fast and visibly on harmful mods, and say what was done.
    Strength: **medium**.
12. **A clear "better than X" story sells a game.** Evidence: beats its rivals 71 (highest in the
    corpus); 135 reviews name Tarkov, 6 thumbs down; *"takes out the worst part of Tarkov, which is the
    cheaters"* (216039510). Dominion could: name what it fixes in the games players already know.
    Strength: **medium**.
13. **A light look can hide a serious game - and that surprise is praised.** Evidence: looks like a joke,
    plays like a real game 39, all thumbs up; the satire lands 31; only one review found the theme wore
    thin. Dominion could: if it uses humour, back it with real depth, and show the depth in trailers.
    Strength: **medium**.
14. **A fun character creator is worth having.** Evidence: 16 times, said of no other game. Dominion
    could: give players a way to make their character their own (looks, colours, a silly option).
    Strength: **weak to medium**.
15. **Runs that fit a short sitting are valued.** Evidence: good in short sittings 18; a game to relax to
    48. Dominion could: keep a standard run short enough for one evening slot, with a clear way out.
    Strength: **medium**.
16. **Watch the camera's view range.** Evidence: the narrow view is a handicap 6; the line-of-sight view
    hides enemies 4. Dominion could: offer a field-of-view setting and make enemies easy to read at the
    edge of the screen. Strength: **weak** (and from a top-down game).
17. **Boss fights must run smoothly.** Evidence: stutter only 4, but one review says every Farm Town boss
    fight stutters (232131285); runs well 7. Dominion could: test frame rate on boss and arena peaks, on
    the host machine too. Strength: **weak**.
18. **Launch praise for "plenty of content" fades.** Evidence: 6.5 per 100 at launch, 1.6 to 2.4 later;
    fair price fell from 8.2 to 2.4 per 100 over the same months. Dominion could: plan content updates for
    the months after launch. Strength: **weak** (late periods are small).

### Limits
- A 10.6% sample; whole-sample shares carry ±2.83%. The launch months (294 and 483 reviews read) are
  solid; the three later periods (123 to 133) carry about ±8.5 to ±8.8 and are direction only.
- Two months (2025-10 and 2025-11) hold 8,113 of the 11,004 reviews; the launch month carries the
  heaviest weight (×13.8).
- 62 reviews (5.3%) were edited later and are counted on the day they were written; how to treat edits is
  open with Rico.
- No patch notes were pulled. Everything about the mod dispute, the collaboration, the studio and the
  publisher comes from reviews and was not checked.
- Hours are those shown on the review; one reviewer says their offline play is not counted.
- Reviews are short: mean 38 words, and 679 of 1,160 (59%) are 15 words or fewer; 486 remarks (21.8%)
  said nothing specific. December 2025 is the exception (mean 71 words), lifted by long protest reviews.
- 37 reviews were written partly or wholly in another language, mostly Chinese, and read in translation;
  one slang phrase (211853631) was left untranslated.
- About 93,000 reviews in other languages (much of it Chinese) were not read.
- The game has no co-op, so the pages say nothing about co-op play itself, only the wish for it.
- The game is under a year old; nothing here speaks to a long live service.

---

## Warframe

**Read:** 3,235 of 302,482 English reviews (1.07%), ±2.16%, written 2013-03 to 2026-09. **89.9% thumbs up**
in the sample (2,908 of 3,233; Steam, all languages: 87%, *Very Positive*).
**Pages:** `findings/warframe-english.md`, `findings/warframe.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 20.

**What it is:** A free-to-play co-op looter shooter in third person, from Digital Extremes, on Steam since
2013-03 - thirteen years of live service, the longest in the corpus. You play missions alone or with a
squad, as what reviewers call a "space ninja", with fast parkour movement. You farm parts to build new
characters (frames) and weapons. Building takes real-world time (crafting timers of 12 hours to 3 days).
Money buys a premium currency (platinum) that can also be traded between players, so a free player can
earn it by selling what they farm. Inventory slots are limited unless you pay or trade. Many players
follow its story. It is an open-ended loop of missions, not runs that end. The pages do not
give the squad size, who hosts, or the engine.

**How close to Dominion:** Close on camera, genre and setting: a third-person sci-fi co-op PvE shooter.
Far on format: there is no run with a start and an end, no extraction, no arena format, and it is
free-to-play with a shop. **Rico stopped this game on 2026-09-24 because it is not a run-based or
extraction game, and resumed it the same week as research; it was later finished.** So read it for the
loop, the grind, the feel and the community, not the format. The pages say nothing about hosting, so
nothing here speaks to a listen server.

### The short version (plain words)
The grind is the game, and players know it. "Too grindy" is the top complaint by far (264 times, 8.2 per
100 reviews), flat for thirteen years, and 223 of those 264 reviews (84%) still recommend the game.
Players call the free-to-play model fair (fair price 198 times, everything can be earned 133), because
money mostly buys time, not power (73 times). The pull to come back is praised (221 times) and feared in
the same breath (74 warn it takes over your life, and 60 of those 73 reviews are still thumbs up). The
first hours are the weak spot and always have been: 267 remarks are about how hard the game is to learn.
Tech is not a problem: crashes come up 4 times in 3,233 reviews.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (one review can say several things), out of 3,233 reviews.

1. **It keeps pulling you back** - 221 times (6.8 per 100 reviews).
2. **The price is fair** - 198 times (6.1 per 100). No one complains about the price as such; every
   price remark is "fair".
3. **Plenty of content** - 142 times (4.4 per 100).
4. **Everything can be earned by playing** - 133 times (4.1 per 100).
5. **It looks great** - 128 times (4.0 per 100).
6. **A welcoming community** - 122 times (3.8 per 100), the ninth most common thing said.
7. **The story is worth following** - 119 times (3.7 per 100), against 22 who call it thin or forgettable.
8. **Builds are deep and varied** - 118 times (3.6 per 100).
9. **Updates keep coming** - 117 times (3.6 per 100).
10. **Much better with friends** - 114 times (3.5 per 100).
11. **Combat hits hard** - 113 times (3.5 per 100).
12. **The grind is long and worth it** - 111 times (3.4 per 100). From 2016 on, about one in three who
    raise the grind say it pays off.
13. **Movement rewards practice** - 69 times (2.1 per 100). 17 of the 69 name parkour, bullet jumping or
    the space ninja.
14. **It beats its rivals** - 67 times (2.1 per 100).
15. **Best free game of its kind** - 62 times (1.9 per 100), all before 2019 (see below).
16. **Each frame plays its own way** - 53 times (1.6 per 100).
17. **A patch made it better** - 51 times (1.6 per 100).
18. **The player market works** - 46 times (1.4 per 100), said of no other game. Against it: works badly
    6, scammers 1. *"you can even earn the platinum used to buy things in the market through trading in
    game items with players."*
19. **The developers listen and change things** - 43 times (1.3 per 100).
20. **It works alone** - 42 times (1.3 per 100).
21. **Made with care** - 41 times (1.3 per 100).
22. **A fair business model** - 37 times (1.1 per 100), said of no other game.
23. **The shop sells looks only** - 35 times (1.1 per 100).
24. **Rewarding once learned** - 34 times (1.1 per 100).
25. **You get to feel overpowered** - 34 times.
26. **Players buy things to support the studio** - 30 times (0.93 per 100), the highest rate in the corpus
    (The Anacrusis 0.82, Arcrunner 0.56).
27. **Runs on modest hardware** - 29 times (against 12 who say it needs a strong PC).
28. **More to do than fighting** - 23 times, 19 of them from 2019 on; said of no other game.
29. **The premium currency can be earned by playing** - 22 times.
30. **Easy to start** - 15 times. **Welcoming to newcomers** - 13 times.
31. **Crafting timers help pace play** - 3 times. *"Crafting times help me pace rather than blowing
    through everything in a few months."* (234735996, 704 hours).
- Also said, without a verdict: 127 reviews call it "okay", "mid" or "decent" (122 of them thumbs up);
  44 say interest comes and goes in waves - *"You never quit warframe, just take longer breaks"*, in some
  form, appears in three reviews.

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said, out of 3,233 reviews. **983 of the 1,646 complaints (59.7%) come from
reviews that still recommend the game.** The median review shows 139 hours; 555 reviews (17.2%) show
1,000 hours or more. The 325 thumbs-down reviews have a median of 62 hours, and 66 of them show 1,000+.

1. **Too grindy** - 264 times (8.2 per 100). **223 of the 264 are thumbs up (84%).** The 41 thumbs-down
   grind reviews have a median of 194 hours, and 11 show over 1,000 - not new players who bounced. At
   1,131 hours (thumbs up, 200 helpful): very grindy, especially when new; *"Money is purely for
   cosmetics. (Or to reduce the grind out of some stuff)"*. At 6,720 hours (thumbs down, 456 helpful):
   *"an extremely heavy grind and a general disrespect to the player's time"*, while still praising the
   visuals. At 1,917 hours (down): *"I shouldn't consider your game a damn second job."* At 60 hours
   (down): *"my progress felt so slow"*. (The pages give no review ids for these.)
2. (82 times a thumbs-down review said nothing specific.)
3. **It takes over your life** (a warning) - 74 times in 73 reviews (2.3 per 100); **60 of the 73 are
   thumbs up.** Said of no other game. *"Relationships are temporary. The Void's embrace lasts forever."*
   (131 helpful). Many are jokes. 9 more say they keep playing while hating it.
4. **Repetitive** - 56 times (1.7 per 100).
5. **Progress held back by real-world timers** - 49 times (1.5 per 100); crafting takes 12 hours to 3
   days.
6. **You need outside guides to understand it** - 43 times (1.3 per 100).
7. **Poorly explained** - 42 times (1.3 per 100).
8. **The part you need may never drop** - 39 times (1.2 per 100). Boosters raise how much you get but
   not the drop odds, so rare stays rare.
9. **The start is slow** - 36 times (1.1 per 100).
10. **Buggy** - 33 times (1.0 per 100).
11. **A mode is not worth playing** - 33 times (1.0 per 100).
12. **Nothing left to chase** - 27 times (0.8 per 100).
13. **A patch made it worse** - 27 times (0.8 per 100).
14. **The story is thin or forgettable** - 22 times (0.7 per 100).
15. **What you can own is capped unless you pay** (inventory slots for frames and weapons) - 21 times
    (0.6 per 100); said of no other game.
16. **Paying affects play** - 16 times (0.5 per 100).
17. **You end up paying sooner or later** - 15 times (0.5 per 100).
18. **A patch weakened what players liked** - 14 times (0.4 per 100).
19. **Will not start at all** - 13 times (0.4 per 100).
20. **Needs a strong PC** - 12 times (0.4 per 100).
21. **You grind for gear you will never use** - 12 times (0.4 per 100).
22. **The shop charges far too much** - 12 times (0.4 per 100).
23. **New players need carrying** by experienced players - 12 times (0.4 per 100).
24. **Locked out of my own account** - 11 times (0.3 per 100).
25. **Too hard alone** - 11 times (0.3 per 100), 9 of them before 2019.
26. **Too much of everything** (systems, currencies, side missions) - 11 times.
27. **It feels like a cash grab** - 10 times.
28. **Support is heavy-handed** (quick to ban, slow to listen) - 9 times.
29. **I have nobody to play with** and the game suffers - 9 times.
30. **The chat filter bans for harmless words** - 6 times.
31. **The end game is only more unlocks** - 5 times.
32. **Story quests must be played alone** - 3 times.
33. **Veterans will not help newcomers** - 3 times.
34. **A much stronger player kills everything first**, so the others never get to play - 3 times.
35. **The levels do not suit the movement** - 2 times.
36. **Crashes** - 4 times in all.
- **Not on the list at all:** cheating, matchmaking, server trouble as a pattern, or price as such.
- Learning the game, all together: 267 remarks. Overwhelming at first (neutral) 72, outside research 43,
  poorly explained 42, slow start 36, against rewarding once learned 34 and easy to start 15. A reviewer
  at 1,389 hours jokes they are almost done with the tutorial.

### How it changed over time
Reviews shrank over thirteen years, and every per-100 number fell with them. Compare things inside one
period; trust a fall across periods only when it is much bigger than the fall in length. Each period
carries about ±3.7 to ±4.5.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 | Mean words per review |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2013-15 | 664 | 90.4% | 116.3 | 57.8 | 67 |
| 2016-18 | 719 | 87.9% | 117.0 | 65.8 | 54 |
| 2019-21 | 720 | 89.6% | 83.6 | 41.2 | 30 |
| 2022-23 | 480 | 91.0% | 65.6 | 42.1 | 30 |
| 2024-26 (to 2026-09) | 650 | 91.4% | 70.0 | 31.8 | 28 |

- **The thumb barely moves in thirteen years**: 87.9% to 91.4% by period; the worst year is 2017 (85.4%),
  the best 2024 and 2026 (92.9%). Praise to complaint holds between 1.6 and 2.2 to 1.
- **Reviews got shorter**: reviews of 15 words or fewer rose from 51% to 69%.
- **The grind complaint does not fall** while everything else does: 8.1 per 100 in 2013-15, 9.6, 7.1,
  9.6, then 6.8. It is the only complaint that holds its rate against the shrinking reviews.
- **"The grind is worth it" appears after the early years**: 0.9 per 100 in 2013-15, then 2.7 to 5.0.
- **Repetitive fell for real**: 3.3 per 100 in 2013-15 and 3.1 in 2016-18, then 0.4 to 1.0 from 2019. In
  the same years "more to do than fighting" appears (19 of its 23 from 2019 on).
- **"It looks great" fell the most**: 5.9 to 0.9 per 100, a six-fold fall against a two-and-a-half-fold
  fall in length. The look aged.
- **Learning the game peaked in 2016-18**: outside research 2.6 per 100 and poorly explained 2.9 per 100,
  falling after.
- **A mode not worth playing peaked in 2016-18** (2.2 per 100).
- **"It takes over your life" peaked in 2022-23** at 5.2 per 100; many are jokes (*"lost gf bcs of the
  grind"*, *"like heroin (and not in a good way)"*).
- **"Best free game of its kind" stops after 2018**: 4.1 and 4.9 per 100, then zero in 1,850 later
  reviews, while "beats its rivals" rises from 1.4 to 3.5. The sample cannot say whether players stopped
  saying it or the filing changed between the two reading sessions.
- **Too hard alone** was mostly an early complaint: 9 of the 11 are from before 2019.
- **Other early-heavy complaints:** capped unless you pay 1.4 per 100 in 2013-15, 0.0 to 0.5 after 2018;
  the shop charges too much 0.9 and 0.7 per 100 before 2019, 0.1 or less after; needs a strong PC
  1.2 per 100 in 2013-15, 0.4 or less after.
- No patch notes were pulled, so the pages do not tie turns to named updates.

### Co-op and online play
- **A co-op game that works alone.** Much better with friends 114; works solo 42, against too hard alone
  11 (9 before 2019) and nobody to play with 9.
- **The co-op praise is about company more than mechanics.** All remarks about co-op design together are
  21, and 3 of those complain that story quests must be played alone.
- **The community is praised far more than blamed.** Welcoming community 122; welcoming to newcomers 13;
  making friends in single figures. Against: veterans will not help newcomers 3; a much stronger player
  kills everything first 3; the chat filter bans for harmless words 6; new players need carrying 12.
- **Teaching is done by other players**, the reviews say: *"ask for help and guidance as often as you can
  early on"*; *"Set your matchmaking to solo for the first few missions"*; *"Don't feel dumb looking up
  plot summaries of missions after you beat them."*
- **Trading between players** is a big part of the game: the player market works 46, works badly 6,
  scammers 1.
- **Not raised as a pattern:** matchmaking, server trouble, cheating, lag. The pages say nothing about
  hosting, joining a mission in progress, dropping out, scaling, bots, friendly fire or crossplay.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** - 113 times; **feeling overpowered** - 34.
- **Movement is praised as a skill to master** - 69 times, 17 naming parkour, bullet jumping or the space
  ninja. 2 say the levels do not suit the movement.
- **Each frame plays its own way** - 53; **builds deep and varied** - 118 (7.8 per 100 in 2013-15).
- Overall the game is described through its progression more than its combat.
- The pages say nothing about the camera, controls, stuns or how readable enemies are.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- The pages say little here. Too hard alone 11 (mostly before 2019); a much stronger player kills
  everything first 3; new players need carrying 12.
- **The end game:** nothing left to chase 27; the end game is only more unlocks 5; you grind for gear you
  will never use 12.
- Bosses and enemy variety are not described on the pages, except one grind quote about farming *"the
  same one type of enemy, boss, or mission"*.

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Unlocking is a third of all game-design talk**: 754 remarks (320 praise, 421 complaint), more than
  everything said about money.
- **The grind loop, from four sides:** too grindy 264 (84% thumbs up); worth it 111; paying only shortens
  the grind 73; takes over your life 74.
- **Money buys time, not power.** The 73 "paying only shortens the grind" remarks range from boosters that
  raise the amount you get but not the drop odds (thumbs up) to the moment *"you do not want to spend
  another week farming the same one type of enemy, boss, or mission"* and pay (thumbs down) - the same
  fact, a different verdict.
- **Real-world timers are the sharpest edge**: 49 complaints (12 hours to 3 days), against 3 who say the
  timers help pace them (234735996).
- **Everything can be earned** - 133. **Premium currency earnable by playing** - 22.
- **Randomness:** the part you need may never drop 39; boosters do not change the odds.
- **The start:** slow start 36; overwhelming at first 72; needs outside research 43; poorly explained 42;
  rewarding once learned 34.
- **The end:** nothing left to chase 27; only more unlocks 5; gear you will never use 12.
- **Inventory caps:** what you can own is capped unless you pay, 21. The neutral "what not to spend on"
  advice (22) gives the same tip 8 times: *"Spend your free plat on slots"*.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **There are no runs** with a start and an end; this game is an open loop of missions.
- **It keeps pulling you back** - 221. **Interest comes and goes in waves** - 44. **Keeps playing while
  hating it** - 9. **Takes over your life** - 74.
- **Plenty of content** - 142 (peak 6.0 per 100 in 2019-21). **Too much of everything** - 11.
- **Repetitive** - 56, falling for real after 2019 as **more to do than fighting** (23) grew.
- **A mode is not worth playing** - 33.
- **Nothing left to chase** - 27.

### Money and price
Dominion is not free-to-play, so most of this is context. It is here because it is most of what the
reviews say.

| Side | What players said | Times |
|---|---|---|
| **Fair** | fair price 198, everything earnable 133, a fair business model 37, looks only 35, buy to support the studio 30, premium currency earnable 22, the player market works 46 | **501** |
| **Neutral** | paying only shortens the grind 73, advice on what not to spend on 22, unclear 16 | **111** |
| **Unfair** | capped unless you pay 21, paying affects play 16, you end up paying 15, the shop charges far too much 12, feels like a cash grab 10, others | **103** |

- **Within the shop talk, praise outnumbers complaint 134 to 103.**
- **The player market makes the premium currency earnable**: one reviewer says the studio's platinum
  prices are very high but player prices are rock bottom.
- **The complaints are about slots and prices, not power**: paying affects play is 16 in 3,233 reviews.
  The four shop complaints (capped, pay affects play, you end up paying, too expensive) come to 64 - less
  than a quarter of the grind complaint.
- **Buying to support the studio** - 30 (0.93 per 100), the highest in the corpus.
- **The fight about money in this game is a fight about time.**

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Engineering is 3.9% of everything said (253 times). **Crashes: 4 times in 3,233 reviews.**
- Buggy 33; will not start at all 13; needs a strong PC 12 against runs on modest hardware 29; locked
  out of my own account 11.
- Support is heavy-handed - 9.
- 6 reviewers say the hours Steam shows are not their real time played.
- The engine is not named.

### The studio, updates and community
- **Updates keep coming** - 117 (3.6 per 100). **A patch made it better** 51; **the developers listen**
  43. Against: **a patch made it worse** 27; **a patch weakened what players liked** 14.
- **The community is a strength**: welcoming 122, the ninth most common thing said.
- **Support and moderation:** heavy-handed support 9; the chat filter bans for harmless words 6.
- **Live-service problems seen only here** (named on the page, no counts given): the lore rewritten, the
  early game left behind, returning players with no way to catch up, an interface that keeps changing.
- **Story is a real draw** in this game: worth following 119, against thin 22.

### What players asked for
The pages give no list of requests; these are the wishes behind the complaints, with their counts.
- **Less grind, or a faster way through it** - 264.
- **Better teaching in the game** - 267 remarks on learning it (outside research 43, poorly explained
  42, slow start 36, overwhelming 72); tips that players give each other instead.
- **No or shorter crafting timers** - 49.
- **More variety** - repetitive 56; modes worth playing 33.
- **Something to chase at the end** - 27; more than unlocks 5.
- **More slots without paying** - 21.
- **Story quests playable in co-op** - 3.
- **Gentler moderation and support** - chat filter 6, heavy-handed support 9.
- **Patches that do not weaken favourites** - 14.

### Only in this game
- **156 of the 548 kinds of remark used here appear in no other game**; 119 new kinds were built while
  reading it, and 89 of the 156 were seen only once. The most used:
  - It takes over your life - 74.
  - The player market works - 46.
  - Interest comes and goes in waves - 44.
  - A fair business model - 37.
  - More to do than fighting - 23.
  - Advice on what not to spend on - 22.
  - What you can own is capped unless you pay - 21.
  - You end up paying sooner or later - 15.
  - You grind for gear you will never use - 12.
  - Too much of everything - 11.
  - Locked out of my own account - 11.
  - Keeps playing while hating it - 9.
  - Support is heavy-handed - 9.
- **The pull read as a harm**: other games' players use addiction words as praise; Warframe's write them
  as a warning and still recommend it.
- **A thirteen-year live service** brings things no other game has had time to do: lore rewritten, the
  early game left behind, returning players with no catch-up, an interface that keeps changing.
- **The top complaint that does not move the thumb**: the cross-game page calls Warframe "the flat
  line" - a complaint built into the design and accepted by the people who stay.

### For Dominion - our reading
The findings page's own "For Dominion" line, with Rico's stop reason in mind: a grind is tolerated when
everything can be earned and the reward is visible; a tutorial that lags the systems is a complaint for
years; a co-op game praised for its company also has to work alone; movement that rewards practice is
named by name. The run, extraction and arena questions Dominion needs answered are not in this game.

1. **A long grind is accepted when everything can be earned and the pay-off is visible.** Evidence: too
   grindy 264, but 223 of them thumbs up (84%); everything can be earned 133; the grind is worth it 111;
   the thumb flat at 87.9% to 91.4% for thirteen years. Dominion could: make all between-run unlocks
   earnable in play, and show clear progress bars toward each one. Strength: **strong**.
2. **Respect the player's time, even for fans.** Evidence: a thumbs-down at 6,720 hours with 456 helpful votes
   names *"a general disrespect to the player's time"*; *"a damn second job"* (1,917 hours); the 41
   thumbs-down grind reviews have a median of 194 hours. Dominion could: cap how long any single unlock
   takes, and avoid farming the same mission over and over for one part. Strength: **strong**.
3. **Teach the game inside the game; a tutorial that lags the systems is a complaint for years.**
   Evidence: 267 remarks on learning it; overwhelming 72, outside research 43, poorly explained 42, slow
   start 36; peak in 2016-18; players do the teaching instead. Dominion could: explain each system when it
   first appears, in plain words, and test with new players at every big update. Strength: **strong**.
4. **Avoid real-world timers.** Evidence: 49 complaints (12 hours to 3 days) against 3 who like them.
   Dominion could: tie unlocks to play, not to clock time. Strength: **medium**.
5. **Random drops need a limit on bad luck.** Evidence: the part you need may never drop 39; boosters do
   not change the odds; gear you will never use 12. Dominion could: add bad-luck protection or a way to
   craft or buy the missing part. Strength: **medium**.
6. **Give the end game something to chase besides more unlocks.** Evidence: nothing left to chase 27; the
   end game is only more unlocks 5. Dominion could: plan harder run types, high-stakes extraction or
   rotating challenges for players who have unlocked most things. Strength: **medium**.
7. **Movement that rewards practice gets praised by name.** Evidence: 69 times, 17 naming parkour or
   bullet jumping; 2 say the levels do not suit the movement. This is a third-person game, like Dominion.
   Dominion could: give its movement a skill ceiling (chained slides, jumps, dodges), and build arenas
   that reward it. Strength: **medium to strong**.
8. **Combat weight and feeling strong are core praise.** Evidence: combat hits hard 113; feeling
   overpowered 34; each frame plays its own way 53. Dominion could: give each class a clearly different
   way to play and strong hit feedback. Strength: **medium**.
9. **A co-op game praised for its company still has to work alone.** Evidence: much better with friends
   114; works solo 42 against too hard alone 11 and nobody to play with 9; story quests solo-only drew 3
   complaints. Dominion could: let every mode be played solo or in a group, and never lock story to one.
   Strength: **medium**.
10. **A welcoming community can be designed for.** Evidence: welcoming community 122 (ninth most common);
    teaching done by other players; against: new players need carrying 12, a stronger player kills
    everything first 3, veterans will not help 3. Dominion could: reward veterans for helping newcomers
    (bonus loot for carrying a new player), and stop very strong players from emptying a newcomer's run.
    Strength: **medium**.
11. **If anything is sold, sell time or looks, never power.** Evidence: paying only shortens the grind
    73; looks only 35; paying affects play just 16; buying to support the studio 30, the highest in the
    corpus. Dominion is not free-to-play, but any cosmetic store or DLC should follow this. Strength:
    **medium** (different price model).
12. **Do not cap what players can own behind payment.** Evidence: capped unless you pay 21; the most
    repeated spending tip is *"Spend your free plat on slots"* (8 times). Dominion could: give a generous
    stash and loadout space from the start. Strength: **weak to medium**.
13. **The pull is praised, but it can be read as harm.** Evidence: keeps pulling you back 221; takes over
    your life 74 (60 of 73 still thumbs up); keeps playing while hating it 9; interest comes in waves 44.
    Dominion could: make runs with natural stopping points, and welcome back players who take a break.
    Strength: **medium**.
14. **Bring returning players back up to speed.** Evidence: the page names "returning players with no
    catch-up" and "the early game left behind" as live-service problems seen only here (no counts).
    Dominion could: plan catch-up for players who return after a season. Strength: **weak**.
15. **Variety beyond fighting cuts "repetitive".** Evidence: repetitive fell from 3.3 to 0.4-1.0 per 100
    after 2019, while "more to do than fighting" (23) grew. Dominion could: mix objectives inside runs
    (carry, defend, hack, escort) beside the fights. Strength: **medium**.
16. **Every mode must be worth playing.** Evidence: a mode is not worth playing 33 (peak 2.2 per 100 in
    2016-18). Dominion could: ship fewer modes, each one tested for fun. Strength: **medium**.
17. **Too many systems works against the game.** Evidence: too much of everything 11; overwhelming at
    first 72. Dominion could: keep the number of currencies and systems small. Strength: **weak**.
18. **Steady updates help; patches that weaken favourites hurt.** Evidence: updates keep coming 117;
    developers listen 43; a patch made it better 51 against made it worse 27 and weakened favourites 14.
    Dominion could: when a weapon or build must be weakened, explain why and offer something back.
    Strength: **medium**.
19. **A good-looking game ages.** Evidence: "it looks great" fell from 5.9 to 0.9 per 100, six-fold, far
    more than the fall in review length. Dominion could: plan visual refreshes for a long life, and lean
    on art direction more than raw detail. Strength: **weak**.
20. **Story can be a draw even in a looter.** Evidence: worth following 119 against thin 22. Dominion
    could: tell its story between runs, not by forcing solo story missions. Strength: **weak to medium**
    (a looter with a long-told story; Dominion's story needs are not tested here).
21. **Solid tech is noticed by its absence of complaints.** Evidence: crashes 4 in 3,233; runs on modest
    hardware 29 against needs a strong PC 12; but will not start 13 and locked out of account 11. Dominion
    could: keep minimum specs modest and make sign-in and account recovery simple. Strength: **weak to
    medium**.
22. **Moderation that is too strict costs goodwill.** Evidence: the chat filter bans for harmless words 6;
    heavy-handed support 9. Dominion could: if it has chat, filter rather than ban, and give a human
    appeal. Strength: **weak**.
23. **Trading between players can make rare things reachable.** Evidence: the player market works 46,
    works badly 6, scammers 1. Dominion could: consider letting teammates trade or hand over drops inside
    a run. Strength: **weak** (a free-to-play market is not Dominion's model).

### Limits
- A 1.07% sample; whole-sample shares carry ±2.16%; each period about ±3.7 to ±4.5; single months are
  direction only (weights up to ×591 in 2018-11).
- The launch is barely covered: 5 reviews from 2013-03, out of 460.
- Reviews shrank from 67 words to 28 on average, so most falls in per-100 rates measure that, not opinion.
  Only falls much bigger than that (repetitive, looks great) are read as real.
- 581 reviews (18.0%) were edited later and are counted on the day they were written; how to treat edits
  is open with Rico.
- Read in two sessions, before and after Rico's stop (batches 1-14, 700 reviews from 2013-03 to 2016-02;
  then batches 15-64). "Best of its kind" vanishing after 2018 may be a change in filing, not in players.
- No patch notes were pulled; update names and dates come from reviews.
- Hours are those shown on the review, which may be total hours at the time of the pull; 6 reviewers say
  the hours shown are wrong.
- 1,382 remarks (21.2%) said nothing specific.
- About 381,000 reviews in other languages were not read.
- The game has no runs, no extraction and no arenas, so it cannot answer the questions Dominion most
  needs answered. It is free-to-play, unlike Dominion.

---

## Risk of Rain 2

**Read:** 1,885 of 239,309 English reviews (0.79%), ±2.64%, written 2019-03 to 2026-09.
**95.6% thumbs up** in the sample (Steam, all languages: 93.8%, *Very Positive*, 352,630 reviews).
**Pages:** `findings/risk-of-rain-2-english.md`, `findings/risk-of-rain-2.md`, `findings/cross-game.md`
section 19.

**What it is:** A third-person co-op roguelite shooter from Hopoo Games, published by Gearbox. Early
access from 2019-03 (opened 2019-03-28), 1.0 in 2020-08. The 3D sequel to *Risk of Rain* (2013).
Each run starts weak; you pick up items (some from gambling shrines) until your character is far too
strong, while a clock makes the enemies harder the longer you take; then you die and start again.
Survivors (characters) are unlocked over time. Co-op is hosted on a player's machine (the pages show
this through the disconnect and host-leaving complaints). Paid game with three paid expansions the
reviews name: *Survivors of the Void* (2022-03), *Seekers of the Storm* (2024-08) and *Alloyed
Collective* (2025-11) - dates from reviews and review volume, not patch notes. Gearbox bought the
series from Hopoo in 2022 (the reviews date it). Mods are common. The pages do not give the largest group size or the engine.

**How close to Dominion:** Close in shape: third-person, co-op, sci-fi, runs that build power and
end, a player hosts the game. It differs in having no extraction (the run ends when you die or
win). It is the best example in the corpus of a run loop players cannot put down.

### The short version (plain words)
The most-recommended game in the queue (95.6% up), and its fans mostly do not say why - 47% of what
they write says nothing specific. When they do, it is four things: it pulls you back like an
addiction (172 times), it is better with friends (88), each run turns you into a god (73), and the
music (71). Complaints are rare - none reaches 1 per 100 reviews - and fell for five years. Then in
2024, after the new owner's expansion launched broken (reviewers say), complaints doubled and the
thumb fell from about 98% to about 93%, the biggest drop the game ever had, with nothing about the
design changed.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said in 1,885 reviews; "per 100" means times per 100 reviews.
1. **It keeps pulling you back** - 172 times, 9.1 per 100, the highest in the corpus. Addiction words:
   *crack* (six times), *drugs*, *doom scrolling*, *one more loop*, *how do I have 40 hours on this
   game*. Thumb up every time but one.
2. **Much better with friends** - 88 times, 4.7 per 100 (7.7 in early access, 2.9 in 2025-26).
3. **The run makes you a god** - 73 times, 3.9 per 100; 35 reviews use the word *god*. *"start slow,
   gain power, become god, die anyway, do it all again"*; *"everything dies without you inputting any
   movement, attacks or abilities - that's Risk of Rain at its peak."* Peaks at 6.9 per 100 after 1.0.
4. **The music fits** - 71 times, 3.8 per 100. The only praise in the corpus that rises for seven
   years (3.3 to 6.0 per 100). The composer, Chris Christodoulou, is named more than any developer in
   the corpus; *"and his music was electric"* is quoted eleven times in 2025-26.
5. **A true sequel to the first game** - 44 times (8.5 per 100 in early access, 0.0 by 2025-26).
6. **Hard in a good way** - 44 times, 2.3 per 100.
7. **Unlocking things feels satisfying** - 39 times, 2.1 per 100.
8. **Each survivor plays its own way** - 37 times, 2.0 per 100. Another 60 reviews just name a
   favourite survivor (*Acrid*, *Loader*, *Huntress*, *Railgunner*, *MUL-T*) and say nothing else.
9. **It works alone** - 34 times, 1.8 per 100.
10. **Mods extend the game** - 31 times, 1.6 per 100.
11. **The price is fair** - 30 times, 1.6 per 100 (against 2 who say it is too high).
12. **Builds are deep and varied** - 29 times, 1.5 per 100.
13. **The randomness keeps it fresh** - 26 times, 1.4 per 100. Another 24 name a gamble and say
    nothing (*Shrine of Chance* five times, *let's go gambling*, *just one more lunar roll*).
14. **The DLC is fair** - 26 times, 1.4 per 100 (0.0 in early access, 2.5 in 2024). *Alloyed
    Collective* got three "worth it" and no thumbs-down.
15. **Good value while unfinished** - 18 times; 4.9 per 100 in early access, zero after 1.0.
16. **It looks great** - 16 times. **A patch fixed what mattered** - 16 times (2.2 per 100 in early
    access). **Clear difficulty steps** - 15 times.
17. **Deep once learned** - 13 times. **The world is worth exploring** - 12 times (the lore is never
    argued with).
18. **The controls are tight and clear** - 11 times, 2.5 per 100 in early access, then zero.
19. **Updates arrive regularly** - 11 times.
20. **The new owner is accepted / has earned trust** - 6 times. *"Gearbox had me scared with SOTS,
    but they fixed it and made the practically bugless Alloyed Collective; they've earned my trust."*
    A patch made it better - 4 times.
21. **The timer is the thrill** - 4 times here (and 3 in Rogue Core). **The action never stops** - 3
    times.

Also: 948 thumbs-up reviews say nothing but *good*, *peak*, *yes* or a weather joke; 34 copy the same
meme text. The bare thumbs-up grows from 24% of remarks in early access to 42% in 2025-26.

### What players complained about, most to least
299 complaint remarks in 1,885 reviews, **173 of them (57.9%) from people who recommend the game.**
1. **Thumbs down with nothing said** - 22 times (*no*, *bad*, *go away*); doubles in 2024.
2. **The effects block your view** - 14 times. Written as a joke 12 times in 14 (*vision is
   overrated anyway*; *I don't know what killed me, would recommend*; *what is happening on my screen?
   10/10*), and thumbs up on 12 of 14. Twice a real complaint: *chaos for the sake of chaos*;
   *"after some point it's just an epileptic crisis"*.
3. **Teammates take the items** - 12 times, from people who stayed (11 of 12 thumbs up). *"I really
   wish my teammates would stop taking all the items"*; *"NO ITEM SHARING IN MULTIPLAYER... many mods
   fix this."* As old as the game, the only complaint early access made more than later years.
4. **The new owner puts me off** - 11 times (zero before 2022, 1.7 per 100 in 2024). *"For as long as
   Gearbox has the IP I could never recommend this."* Five more hold a grudge under a thumbs-up.
5. **Frequent disconnects** - 10 times, almost all since 2024 (1.2 per 100 in 2024).
6. **A patch made it worse** - 10 times, 1.7 per 100 in 2024. *"I don't even have the DLC and they've
   introduced bugs from it."*
7. **Repetitive** - 9 times.
8. **Unstable frame rate** - 8 times (1.1 per 100 after 1.0).
9. **One-hit kills** - 7 times (rising to 1.0 per 100 in 2025-26). Only one says which enemy (*one-shot
   by Brass Contraptions*).
10. **Hard to find the way onward (the teleporter)** - 6 times.
11. **Buy it on sale only** - 6. **The item you need may never drop** - 6 (three of the six are about
    unlocks gated on a chance spawn, not items). **Too easy** - 6 (all thumbs-down or *2 ez*; rising to 1.0 per 100 in 2025-26).
    **The DLC is not worth it** - 6, all 2024-26.
12. **Luck decides the outcome** - 5. **Grindy** - 5.
13. **Cannot save and come back later** - 4 (0.8 per 100 in early access). **Buggy** - 4. **The music
    is annoying** - 4. **A crash loses the run** - 4. **Nothing left to chase** - 4.
14. **Poorly explained; no tutorial** - 4 (all in 2025-26, 1.0 per 100). One reviewer holds three
    late complaints at once: *"Too easy, until those moments where it isn't and you die in a second...
    also the tutorial doesn't work, or maybe even exist."*
15. **Two each:** the levels are too small; the first stages of every run are dull; the strongest
    items are locked behind hard challenges (*"so the player who needs them most can't get them"*);
    mods fixed what the patch broke; stuck with the worst starter survivor (*"Commando is extremely
    boring and the game forces you to play him for hours"*); a disconnect loses the run.
16. **One each:** the host leaving ends everyone's run; high latency; the run becomes an idle game -
    *"is someone really playing the game when there is no point to user inputs? Yet I can't stop
    playing it"* (223339415, 247 hours, thumbs down).
17. **Hated enemies:** 14 reviews name one and say *remove it* (blind pests, wisps, Elder Lemurians,
    Brass Contraptions); only one says why.
18. **The 2025 terms change** (see "The studio"): three reviews, two of them among the most-helped
    thumbs-down.

### How it changed over time
| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Early access (2019-03 to 2020-07) | 364 | 96.2% | 119.8 | 22.5 |
| 1.0 (2020-08 to 2021-12) | 376 | 98.4% | 115.7 | 13.0 |
| 2022 to 2023 | 489 | 96.1% | 96.7 | 9.2 |
| 2024 (Seekers of the Storm) | 241 | 92.9% | 90.0 | 18.3 |
| 2025-01 to 2026-09 | 415 | 93.5% | 94.5 | 19.0 |

- **No launch spike and no cliff.** 1,000 to 9,000 reviews a month for seven and a half years. The
  biggest months are events: 2020-11 (9,159, anniversary update and autumn sale), 2020-08 (8,867,
  1.0), 2020-04 (7,132, an update and the first lockdown month), 2019-11 (6,992), and the expansions
  2022-03 (6,863), 2024-08 (4,906), 2025-11 (6,003).
- **Complaints fell for five years** as early-access bugs and missing content were fixed, **then
  doubled in 2024**: the owner (1.7 per 100), disconnects (1.2), patch made it worse (1.7), DLC not
  worth it (1.2) - all zero before 2022. 2024-08 and 2024-09 are the two lowest months in 91 (76.2%
  and 80.0% up).
- **The repair did not bring the complaint line down.** In 2025-26 fixes outnumber "made it worse" 2
  to 1 on tiny numbers, the thumb is 93-96% by month, but complaints stay at 19 per 100. The page's
  reading: those put off did not come back to say so.
- **Early access was the best-explained period.** Good value while unfinished 4.9 per 100, waiting for
  promised content 4.1, fixed what mattered 2.2, tight controls 2.5 - all near zero after 1.0.
- **What rose:** the music (3.3 to 6.0), DLC fair (0 to 2.2), the owner (0 to 1.0 via 1.7),
  disconnects (0 to 1.0), too easy / one-hit kills / no tutorial (each 0 to 1.0), other languages under
  the English tag (0.3 to 1.4).
- **What fell:** sequel praise (8.5 to 0), better with friends (7.7 to 2.9), pulls you back (11.5 to
  6.5), the god run (6.9 to 1.7), hard in a good way (3.2 to 1.4), works alone (3.0 to 1.0), price
  fair (2.7 to 0.5). The page reads this as the later reviewer describing less, not the game changing.

### Co-op and online play
- Better with friends 88; works alone 34.
- **Items go to whoever picks them up, and teammates take things** (the page calls it per-player loot
  in a shared arena) - 12 complaints over seven years; mods fix it.
- **Player hosting shows its cost late:** frequent disconnects 10 (since 2024), a disconnect loses the
  run 2, the host leaving ends everyone's run 1, high latency 1, a crash loses the run 4.
- **Cannot save and come back** - 4.
- One review says the weaker teammate resents the power gap (one sighting).
- Friendly fire: one line in 1,885. Crossplay: one Linux crossplay bug.
- No matchmaking, cheating or stranger complaints are reported.

### Combat, movement and feel
- The god run 73: weak at the start, unstoppable by the end.
- Tight, clear controls 11 (early access).
- **Screen clutter:** the effects block your view 14; one review asks for an epilepsy warning
  (152614012: *"there should definitely be an epilepsy warning somewhere in the game"*).
- When power outgrows the game, play stops needing input (1 review, 247 hours).
- One review: an attack you cannot see coming (one sighting).

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- Hard in a good way 44; too easy 6; clear difficulty steps 15; 30 raise difficulty and say nothing
  (*Risk of Pain*).
- One-hit kills 7. 44 reviews name an enemy and say nothing; 14 name a hated enemy.
- **The clock as difficulty:** the late reviewer says both *too easy* and *you die in a second* - the
  timer's climb read as a spike by someone who has not learned it. 4 say the clock is the thrill.
- The first stages of each run are dull - 2 (plus 1 in another game).

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- Satisfying unlocks 39; deep builds 29; deep once learned 13. Grindy 5; nothing left to chase 4.
- The strongest options are locked behind hard challenges - 2.
- Stuck with the worst starter survivor - 2 (and one review defends him).
- Randomness: 62 remarks. Keeps it fresh 26; a gamble named 24; never drops 6 (three about unlocks);
  luck decides 5.
- Item complaints are tiny: some items are trash (1), stacking falls off (1).

### Runs, content and replay value
- Pulls you back 172 - the strongest in the corpus.
- Repetitive 9. Levels too small 2. The early stages of each run are dull 2.
- Hard to find the way onward 6.
- Cannot save mid-run 4; a crash or disconnect loses the run.
- Mods extend the game 31.

### Money and price
- Price fair 30 against too high 2; buy on sale only 6.
- DLC fair 26 (much of it the first expansion) against DLC not worth it 6 (all about the 2024
  expansion, which both sides say shipped broken).
- One review each: the DLC price as a caveat; the DLC does not fit the theme; buying copies for
  friends.
- Good value while unfinished 18 (early access).

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Tech is 3.1% of all remarks, the lowest of any large game.
- Unstable frame rate 8; buggy 4; a crash loses the run 4; disconnects 10.
- Platform lines only from 2025: Steam Deck broken (1), Mac through a compatibility layer (1), a
  Linux crossplay bug (1). One 16 GB RAM line; one Mac request.

### The studio, updates and community
- **The owner change.** Gearbox is named in 35 reviews. First line on its own date: 2023-01-07, *"The
  devs sold out to Gearbox, the game has no future."* Owner puts me off 11; owner accepted 6; grudge
  under a thumbs-up 5. Reviewers carry blame from the publisher's other games (*Aliens: Colonial
  Marines*, *Borderlands*) and jab its chief executive.
- **The 2024 expansion:** *"unplayable and basically ruined"* (2024-08-31); *"You need a roadmap for bug
  fixes because you did a quick cash grab on a DLC that was nowhere near ready"* (189635801, 6 helpful).
  Then fixes: *"was good, then bad, now it's good again"*; *"the game is now in good hands, and I'm
  willing to wait and see"* (181186472).
- **The 2025 terms change (EULA), in three steps, as reviewers tell it:** *"Nice EULA change, tracking
  user information such as passwords, addresses, phone numbers, and IP addresses"* (193373656, 10
  helpful); *"Added EULA that I have no intent of accepting after I bought the game. Now I cannot play
  the game nor refund it"* (196109434, 9 helpful); then *"Changing my review as they seem to have
  removed/not acted on their new EULA"* (196660257). The wall went up, and came down.
- Updates regular 11; fixed what mattered 16; made it better 4; made it worse 10.
- Mods extend the game 31; mods fixed what the patch broke 2.
- The music and composer are the community's quotable thing; a copied meme in 34 reviews.
- Story: 23 remarks in 1,885, 12 of them "worth exploring", none against.

### What players asked for
- Item sharing in multiplayer (in the 12 "teammates take the items" complaints).
- A tutorial and explanations (4, all 2025-26).
- Remove hated enemies (14 name one).
- An epilepsy warning (1).
- Save and come back mid-run (4).
- Bigger levels with more to find (2).
- A different starter survivor (2).
- A Mac version (1).

### Only in this game
- **The music rising for seven years** - the only praise line in the corpus that does.
- **The new owner accepted** (built here) and **a terms wall added after purchase** (its third sighting
  here).
- **The strongest options locked behind challenges**, **stuck with the worst starter**, **the early
  stages of every run are dull**, **the levels are too small**, **the host leaving ends everyone's
  run**, **a crash loses the run**, **mods fixed what the patch broke** - built during this game's read
  (8 of the 240 kinds of remark used here appear in no other game).
- **The timer as the thrill** and **the action never stops** - praise twins built here.
- **Addiction words as praise**, at the highest rate in the corpus.

### For Dominion - our reading
1. **Make the run loop short and "one more go".**
   Evidence: pulls you back 172 (9.1 per 100, the corpus high); *one more loop*.
   Dominion could: keep runs short enough to start another, put the restart one button away, and end
   each run with a hook (a new unlock, a near-miss).
   Strength: **strong**.
2. **Let players go from weak to god inside one run - but keep them playing.**
   Evidence: the god run 73; 35 use the word *god*; one 247-hour reviewer says it turns into an idle
   game.
   Dominion could: let power snowball within a run, but keep the end of a run (extraction, the arena
   finale) asking for skill - rising threats, a timer, a final fight that power alone does not win.
   Strength: **strong**.
3. **Cap screen clutter and add an effects option and a seizure warning.**
   Evidence: effects block your view 14 (the second complaint); two real complaints mention fits; one
   asks for an epilepsy warning.
   Dominion could: let players turn down teammates' effects, keep enemy attacks readable above the
   clutter, ship a photosensitivity warning.
   Strength: **medium**.
4. **Decide loot sharing on purpose; do not let a fast teammate take everything.**
   Evidence: teammates take the items 12, steady for seven years, fixed by mods.
   Dominion could: give each player their own drops, or show who claimed what, or split extraction
   loot fairly.
   Strength: **medium** (small count, but steady, and it matters more with extraction loot).
5. **On a player-hosted game, protect the run from drops.**
   Evidence: disconnects 10 (since 2024), a disconnect loses the run 2, the host leaving ends
   everyone's run 1, a crash loses the run 4, cannot save and come back 4.
   Dominion could: let a dropped player rejoin a running game; consider host migration or at least save
   each player's run state when the host leaves; allow suspend-and-resume.
   Strength: **medium** (small counts, but exactly Dominion's listen-server risk).
6. **Music is worth real money.**
   Evidence: the music 71, the only praise in the corpus that keeps rising; the composer named more
   than any developer.
   Dominion could: hire a strong composer, build tracks that rise with the run, make them quotable.
   Strength: **strong**.
7. **Never change the deal after people bought it.**
   Evidence: the terms wall (*"now I cannot play the game nor refund it"*, 9 helpful) and the data
   complaint (10 helpful) were two of the most-helped thumbs-down; the owner's name cost two points.
   Dominion could: keep terms, data use and accounts the same as on day one; do not add a sign-in or
   consent screen later.
   Strength: **medium**.
8. **Ship expansions finished; a broken one costs more than it earns.**
   Evidence: 2024 complaints doubled (9.2 to 18.3 per 100); patch made it worse 1.7 per 100; DLC not
   worth it 6 against DLC fair 26; the line never came back down.
   Dominion could: test DLC and big updates on the base game too (one reviewer got bugs without the
   DLC); delay rather than ship broken.
   Strength: **medium**.
9. **Teach new players, and put a good character first.**
   Evidence: no tutorial 4, too easy 6, one-hit kills 7 - all rising together in 2025-26; stuck with
   the worst starter 2; the early stages dull 2; the way onward hard to find 6.
   Dominion could: add a short tutorial run, make the first class fun, mark the exit or objective
   clearly, and make the first minutes of a run interesting.
   Strength: **medium**.
10. **Support mods.**
    Evidence: mods extend the game 31; mods fixed what a patch broke 2; the item-sharing fix is a mod.
    Dominion could: plan for mod support or at least private-match options, and do not break mods
    with every patch.
    Strength: **medium**.
11. **Let randomness feel like a fun gamble, but never gate unlocks on luck.**
    Evidence: randomness keeps it fresh 26, gambles named 24; but never drops 6 (three of them unlocks
    gated on chance), luck decides 5, the strongest items locked behind hard challenges 2.
    Dominion could: put gambling shrines in runs for items, but give unlocks a sure path.
    Strength: **medium**.
12. **A clock that raises the danger can be the thrill.**
    Evidence: the timer is the thrill 4 (plus 3 in Rogue Core, where the clock is also the top
    argument).
    Dominion could: test a rising-threat clock in runs, with clear warnings as it climbs.
    Strength: **weak**.
13. **Early access works when it is honest and fast.**
    Evidence: good value while unfinished 4.9 per 100 in early access; fixed what mattered 2.2; tight
    controls 2.5; complaints fell 22.5 to 9.2 per 100 over five years.
    Dominion could: if it goes into early access, sell it as unfinished, fix the top issues fast, and
    say what is coming.
    Strength: **medium**.
14. **Characters are identity; make each one play differently.**
    Evidence: each survivor plays its own way 37; 60 just name a favourite.
    Dominion could: give each class a distinct feel and a fan-favourite personality.
    Strength: **medium**.
15. **If Dominion is ever sold or partnered, the partner's name becomes part of the review.**
    Evidence: the owner change was the biggest event in this game's score.
    Strength: **weak** for design; noted for business.
16. **Watch the text, not just the score.**
    Evidence: 47% of remarks say nothing; the 2024 rise shows first in specific complaints.
    Strength: **medium**.

### Limits
- The thinnest sample in the queue: 0.79% at ±2.64%; single months are 20 to 39 reviews and only show
  direction. Read the five periods.
- 1.42 remarks per review and 47% saying nothing specific: counts are built from the half who spoke.
  A mode seen six times is just six times.
- 223 reviews (11.8%, the highest share) were edited later and counted on the day first written. This
  moves the ownership story: 15 of the 35 Gearbox lines are 2024-26 edits sitting on 2019-23 dates.
- The launches are barely covered: 5 reviews from the four-day early-access month, 37 from the 1.0
  month.
- No patch notes were pulled; expansion dates and the EULA story are from reviews and volume.
- English only; about 113,000 reviews in other languages were not pulled; 16 "English" reviews were in
  other languages.
- The pages give no largest group size and no engine.
- The complaint rate is 15.9 per 100 on the game's own pages and in cross-game section 19, but 18.0 in
  the later cross-game recount (after a counting fix); praise to complaint is 6.5 : 1 on its pages and
  5.8 : 1 in the recount. The recount is the one to compare across games.

---

## Remnant II

**Read:** 1,382 of 38,120 English reviews (3.63%), ±3.27%, written 2023-07 to 2026-09.
**83.6% thumbs up** in the sample (Steam, all languages: 82.3%, *Very Positive*, 68,559 reviews).
**Pages:** `findings/remnant-2-english.md`, `findings/remnant-2.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 18.

**What it is:** A third-person co-op "souls-like" shooter from Gunfire Games, published by Gearbox
(the reviews later name Arc Games). Released 2023-07-25 at full price. It is the sequel to *Remnant:
From the Ashes* (2019). Worlds are put together by the computer from set pieces, so two players may
see different areas, bosses and secrets. You pick two classes ("archetypes") at once. Three paid DLCs
are named by reviewers (*The Awakened King*, *The Forgotten Kingdom*, *The Dark Horizon*), plus a
boss rush mode, loadout slots and a region filter that reviewers say came later (not checked; no
patch notes were pulled). Co-op is hosted by one player, who keeps the campaign; others join as
guests (the pages show this through the guest complaints). The pages do not give the largest group size or the engine.

**How close to Dominion:** Very close on the outside: third-person, co-op, sci-fi/fantasy shooter,
and one player hosts - so its online complaints are the ones Dominion is most likely to meet. It differs in being a long campaign with checkpoints rather than
runs, having no extraction, and being hard in the "souls" way.

### The short version (plain words)
Players accept it as a worthy sequel (139 times) and say it is much better with friends (139 times).
The number one complaint is that the best things - whole classes, dungeons, items - are hidden so well
that you need a wiki to find them (61 times, 46 of them from people who still recommend it). That
complaint rose every year, from 2.6 per 100 reviews at launch to 10.0 in 2026, while the game itself
did not change. Players love the builds (98) and the worlds (92), and on PC many could not run it well
without upscaling. The thumb fell from 86.5% at launch to 79.6% in 2025 and 82.4% in 2026.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said in 1,382 reviews; "per 100" means times per 100 reviews.
1. **A worthy sequel; better than the first game** - 139 times, 10.1 per 100. *"An improvement on the
   first in every way."* Falls from 12.7 per 100 at launch to 4.7 in 2026.
2. **Much better with friends** - 139 times, 10.1 per 100. Rises from 7.3 to 12.4; by 2026 one review
   in eight says it.
3. **Builds are deep and varied** - 98 times, 7.1 per 100. *"pair an Engineer turret with Hunter crit
   chains and suddenly you're John Wick in an interdimensional maze"* (201175078).
4. **The world is worth exploring** - 92 times, 6.7 per 100.
5. **It keeps pulling you back** - 75 times, 5.4 per 100.
6. **Hard in a good way** - 71 times, 5.1 per 100 (falls from 6.5 at launch to 2.5 in 2025).
7. **The shooting has punch** - 63 times, 4.6 per 100. One pistol sounds *"like slamming a hammer on a
   solid wood door"* (234976329). Nobody says the guns feel weak.
8. **Memorable special enemies and bosses** - 44 times, 3.2 per 100.
9. **It looks great** - 35 times, 2.5 per 100.
10. **The computer-built worlds keep it varied** - 33 times, 2.4 per 100 (peaks at 4.6 in 2025).
11. **It works alone** - 25 times, 1.8 per 100. One 2025 reviewer beat every difficulty solo
    (206126223, 18 votes).
12. **Clear difficulty steps** - 24 times, 1.7 per 100.
13. **The story is worth following** - 23 times, 1.7 per 100.
14. **It won over someone who avoids this kind of game** - 21 times, 1.5 per 100.
15. **Places that set a mood** - 20 times, 1.4 per 100.
16. **The price is fair** - 19 times, 1.4 per 100 (2.1 in late 2023, 0.0 in 2026).
17. **The atmosphere draws you in** - 17 times. **Plenty of content** - 17 times.
18. **The music fits** - 15 times.
19. **The levels are well built** - 13 times. **The DLC is fair** - 13 times.
20. **Each class plays its own way** - 12 times. **Best of its kind** - 12 times.
21. **The dog** - the world reacting to you is 11 times and almost all of it is the Handler class's
    dog: *"you get a dog which you can pet"* (230565254); *"never got attached to any of the
    characters except the dog"* (218327994). The only character named with affection.
22. **The studio earned my trust** - 11 times.
23. **Secrets as a feature**, from the people who found them: *"finding one by chance, or finally
    solving something after hours of curiosity, the payoff is incredible"* (202701424); *"the community
    maps secrets together"* (144280701). No separate count.
24. **Firing range and loadout slots** named as thoughtful extras (214769098), after players asked for
    a way to save loadouts (4 times). No count for the thanks.
25. Smaller praise with no count on the pages: you do not need to have played the first game; your
    choices change the story; one review calls the final boss their favourite ever (228019753).

Also: 396 thumbs-up reviews say nothing specific; 135 reviews explain the game by naming another one
(*Dark Souls* in 57, *Souls* in 64, Elden Ring 22, Destiny 21, Diablo 7, Borderlands 7, Gears 4) -
*"Dark Souls with guns"*, said as praise and as complaint.

### What players complained about, most to least
1. **The best things are hidden behind a guide** - 61 times, 4.4 per 100. **46 of the 61 are thumbs
   up**, median 49 hours. *"You will play this one with the wiki open on another monitor"*
   (197321698); *"Easter egg simulator"* (213370670); the most-helpful negative review (112 votes):
   *"'wiki-checklist' styled secrets that hide a lot of content"* (204418952).
2. **It falls short of the first game** - 53 times, 3.8 per 100, **30 of them thumbs down**, median 28
   hours (those who accept the sequel: 47 hours). Named losses: armour set bonuses (7 reviews), the
   trait cap that let you max everything (9), Survival mode (6 mentions), the desert biome, the old
   worlds. The most-helpful review in the sample is two words: *"uncap trait points"* (174 votes,
   thumbs up).
3. **Thumbs down with nothing said** - 47 times (rises from 1.1 to 8.8 per 100).
